2a1(mdo): @#//rgyas kyi bya ba ni byas /ngas gnas myed pa'i chos zhi ba /rab tu zhi ba /mchog /gya nom pa /zab pa /mthong bar dka' ba /khong du chud par dka' ba /brtag my-i nus pa /ma brtags
2a2(mdo): pa /rtog ge ba'i spyod yul ma y-in ba /mkhas pa dang /'dzangs pas rig pa /phul du phy-ind par 'phags pa'i bdud rtsi ni bstand /kun dga' bo ngas chos kyi 'khor lo bla na myed pa /dge sbyong ngam /
2a3(mdo): bram ze 'am /bdud dam /lha 'am /tshangs pa'am /gzhan gang gis kyang /chos dang mthun bar 'jig rten du ma bskord pa /lan gsum du bzlas te /rnam pa bcu gnyis su bskor ba ni bskor /ngas
2a4(mdo): chos kyi rnga chen po ni brdungs /ngas chos kyi dung chen po ni bus /ngas chos kyi rgyal mtshan chen po ni bsgreng /ngas chos kyi mar mye chen po ni bus /ngas mun pa chen po ni bsald /ngas stong
2a5(mdo): gsum ni snang bar byas /ngas gru chen po ni bcas /ngas stegs chen po ni btsugs /ngas zam pa chen po ni btags /ngas pha rold 'dong ba mang po ni ston //ngas thard pa'i lam
2a6(mdo): n-i yang dag par bstand /ngas chos kyi char chen po ni phab /ngas lha dang myi rnams ni tshim par byas /ngas 'dul ba rnams n-i btuld /ngas phal kyi rgol ba thams cad ni skrag par byas /
2a7(mdo): ngas mu stegs kyi gnas thams cad ni tshar bcad /ngas bdud kyi stobs ni bcom /ngas bdud kyi gnas ni g.yo bar byas /ngas seng ge'i sgra ni bsgrags /ngas sangs rgyas kyi bya ba ni byas /ngas skyes bu dam pa'i las
2a8(mdo): ni mthar phyung /ngas sngon gyi yi dam ni yongs su rdzogs par byas /ngas chos kyi tshul ni byin kyis brlabs /ngas nyan thos chen po rnams las byed du ni btsud /ma 'ongs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi tshul rgyun myi 'chad par
2a9(mdo): bya ba'i phyir /ngas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams lung ni bstand z-ind te /kun dga' bo mya ngan las 'das pa ma rtogs par /phyis bya ba nga la myed do //de nas tshe dang ldan ba kun dga' bo
2b1(mdo): bcom ldan 'das ky-i gsung rnam pa de lta bu de thos nas /sdug bsngal zhing yid myi bde ste /myi dga' zhing mya ngan chen po'i zug rngu zug par gyurd nas /rab tu ngus te mchi ma zag bzhin du /bcom ldan 'das la 'd-i 
2b2(mdo): skad ces gsold to //bcom ldan 'das rab tu myur bar mya ngan las 'da' zhing /bde bar gshegs pa rab tu myur bar mya nganalas 'da' ste /'jig rten kyi myig rab tu myur bar myi snang bar 'gyur /rab tu myur bar
2b3(mdo): 'jig rten mgon ma mch-is pa dang /skyabs ma mchis pa dang /yongs su 'dren ba ma mchis par 'gyur ro //de nas yang bcom ldan 'das ky-is /tshe dang ldan ba kun dga' bo la bka' stsald pa /kun
2b4(mdo): dga' bo /chog gis mya ngan ma byed /smre sngags ma 'don cig /gang gnas pa dang byung ba dang /'dus byas dang /bsags pa ni rab tu 'j-ig pa'-i chos can yin bas /myi 'j-ig par ga
2b5(mdo): la 'gyur te de ni gnas myed do //kun dga' bo ngas thog ma nyid du khyed la /dga' ba dang /sdug pa dang /y-id du 'ong ba rnams dang /tha dad du 'gyur zhing 'gyes par 'gyur /bral
2b6(mdo): bar 'gyur bye bar 'gyur ro zhes ma bstand tam /kun dga' bo khyod kyis nga la byams pa'i lus ky-i las phan ba dang /bde ba dang /gnyis su myed pa dang /tshad myed pa dang /myi mthun
2b7(mdo): myed pas bsnyen bkur bar byas so //kun dga' bo gang lha dang /my-i dang /lha ma yin gyi 'khor du bcas pa dang /nyan thos dang /rang sangs rgyas su bcas pa la bskald pa 'am /bskal pa las lhag par
2b8(mdo): bsnyen bkur byas pa bas /gang de bzhin gshegs pa la yud tsam cig bsnyen bkur byas pa de /de bas 'bras bu shind tu ches /kun dga' bo khyod mya ngan ma byed cig /smre sngags ma 'don cig //kun dga' bo khyod
2b9(mdo): ky-is de bzh-in gshegs pa la bsnyen bkur byas pa ni /don che ba dang /'bras bu che ba dang /phan yon che ba dang /rgya che ba dang /bdud rtsi dang 'dra ba dang /bdud rtsi 'i dang 'bab pa dang /bdud rtsi 'i mthar 'gyur ba
3a1(mdo): //nas /mya ngan las 'da' ba'i bar du 'gyur ro //de nas tshe dang ldan ba kun dga' bos mchi ma phyis nas /smre sngags 'don cing /shing sa la zung gi bar du de bzhin gshegs pa seng ge ltar glo g.yas pas mnal 
3a2(mdo): ba'i gzims mal bcas so //de nas bcom ldan 'das shing sa la zung ga la ba der gshegs nas /shing sa la zung gi b.radu seng ge ltar glo g.yas bas mnald to //gang gi tshe de bzhin gshegs pa shing sa la zung gi
3a3(mdo): bar du seng ge ltar glo g.yasapas mnal par gyurd pa de 'i tshe /stong gsum gyi stong chen po 'i 'jig rten gyi khams na /sh-ing dang rtswa dang /shing gel ba dang /sman dang /nags tshal tha na sor bzhi tsam yan cad ji snyed
3a4(mdo): yod pa de dag thams cad gang du de bzhin gshegs pa mya ngan las 'da' ba de logs su gzhol /'dud /rab tu 'dud cing mngon par 'dud par gyurd to //stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gi
3a5(mdo): khams na /klung ngam /'bab chu 'am /chu myig gam /mtsho 'am /lteng ka 'am /khron pa'am /rdzing ngam /pad ma dkar po 'bab pa ji snyed yod pa de dag thams cad sangs rgyas kyi mthus
3a6(mdo): byin kyis brlabs pas myi 'bab par gyurd to //stong gsam gyi stong chen po 'i 'jigs rten kyi khams na /bya mang po dang /ri dgas mang po sna tshogs ji snyed yod pa de dag thabs cad kyang sangs
3a7(mdo): rgyas kyi mthus sgra myi 'byin par 'khod cing myi za myi rgyu bar gyurd to //stong gsumagyi stong chen po 'i 'jig rten gyi khams na /nyi ma dang zla ba'i 'od dam /skar ma'i gzugs sam /nor bu 'am /mye 'am /srin
3a8(mdo): bu mye khyer kyi 'od ji snyed yod pa de dag tha mas cad sangs rgyas kyi mthu sa /myi mngon myi snang ste /lam mer ma yin /lhan ner ma yin /lhang nger ma yin bar ma gyurd to //stong gsum gyi stong chen po 'i 'jig rte
3a9(mdo): gyi khams na /sems can dmyal ba chen po'i mye ci snyed yod pa de dag thamasacad kyang sangs rgyas kyi mthus zhi bar gyurd /myi 'bar bar gyurd /myi tsha bar gyurd /myi tshig par gyurd /yongs su myi gdung bar gyurd to //
3b1(mdo): stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams na /gang sems can dmyal ba chen po 'i mye de dag thams cad kyang nye bar zhi zhing bsil bar gyurd to //de nas sems can dmyal ba'i sems can de dag thams cad skad
3b2(mdo): cig de dang /yud tsam de 'i tshe sangs rgyas kyi mthus bde ba thob par gyurd to //dud 'gro'i skye gnas pa'i sems can thams cad kyang /byams pa'i sems dang /phan pa'i sems dang ldan bar gyurd te /gcig la
3b3(mdo): gcig gnod sems myed /rnam par 'tshe ba myed /rnam par 'tho 'tsham ba myed cing gnod pa byed pa myed par gyurd to //gshin rje 'i 'jig rten pa'i seMs can thaMs cad kyang bkres pa dang skom ba myed par gyurd to //sems
3b4(mdo): can thams cad kyang skad cig de dang /yud tsam de'i tshe /sangs rgyas kyi mthus bde pa dang /lus dang sems tshim ba dang /sdug bsngal rgyun chad pa dang /bde ba dang /y-id bde ba mchog dang ldan
3b5(mdo): bar gyurd to //de bzhin gshegs pa seng ge ltar glo g.yas pas mnal ma thag tu stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten ky-i khams na /r-i 'i rgyal po ri rab dang /'khor yug dang /
3b6(mdo): 'khor yug chen po dang /btang zung dang /btang zung chen po dang /spos kyi ngad ldang dang /gangs ri ci snyed yod pa dang /gzhan yang ri nag po rnams dang /thams cad dang ldan ba'i sa
3b7(mdo): chen po dang /rgya mtsho chen po'i 'khor yug gi 'obs kyi mtha' gtugs pa rnam pa drug tu g.yos te /'gul rab tu 'gul /'khrugs rab tu 'khrugs /ldeg rab tu ldeg par gyurd to //thams cad dang
3b8(mdo): ldan ba'i rlung gi dkyil 'khor yang de 'i tshe myi ldang bar gyurd to //de 'i tshe skad cig de dang /yud tsam de na sems can thams cad kyang las byed pa rnams btang bar gyurd te /gny-id myed chod pa myed /sgrib pa myed /
3b9(mdo): rtsom ba myed cing /cang myi zer bar gyurd te /sgra chung /ca co chung zhing yid myi g.yeng bar gyurd te /bcom ldan 'das seng ge ltar glo g.yas pas mnal ma thag tu /stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'j-ig rten gyi khaMs
4a1(mdo): //'d-i na lha dang /klu dang /gnod sbyin dang /dri za dang /lha ma yin dang /nam mkha' lding dang /myi 'am ci dang /lto 'phye chen po dang /brgya byin dang /tshangs pa dang /'j-ig rten skyong ba
4a2(mdo): ci snyed 'khod pa de dag thams cad kyis rang rang gi gnas rnams mog mog por gyurd te /'od myed pa /gzi byin myed par gyurd par shes so //de bzhin gshegs pa seng ge ltar glo g.yas pas mnal ma thag tu /
4a3(mdo): stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten kyi khams 'di na lha dang /klu dang /gnon sbyin dang /dri za dang lha ma yin dang /nam mkha'i lding dang /myi 'am ci dag /lto 'phye chen po dang/brgya byin dang /tshangs pa rang
4a4(mdo): 'jig rten skyong ba ji snyed 'khod pa de dag tham sa cad rang rang gi stan dang skyed mos tshal dang /gzhal myed khang rnams la myi dga' bar gyurd to //sangs rgyas ky-i mthus de dag thams cad 'khor
4a5(mdo): dang bcas par skyo zhing yid byung bar gyurd to //stong gsum gyi stong chen po 'jig rten gi khams 'di na tshangs pa dang /tshangs pa chen po gang dag zil kyis gnon cing /zild kyis myi
4a6(mdo): non pa brgya phrag bcu la dbang sgyur ba /'di snyam du sems sh-ing /bdag cag gis sems can 'di dag sprul /bdag cag gis 'jig rten byas /bdag cag gis 'jig ten spruld to snyam
4a7(mdo): du de ltar shes pa'-i tshangs pa dang /tshangs pa chen po stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gi khams na 'khod pa de dag kyang /rang rang gi stan la mngon par myi dga' bar gyurd to /rang rang gi gnas thams
4a8(mdo): dang ldan ba yang sangs rgyas ky-i mthus /mog mog par gyurd par shes so //de bzhin du tshangs pa gzhan thams cad kyang sangs rgyas kyi mthus rang rang gi gnas rnams mog mog por gyurd par shes te /rang rang
4a9(mdo): gy-i gnas rnams la myi dga' bar gyurd to//'j-ig rten skyong ba dang /dbang phyug chen po dang /gnas gtsang ma rnams kyang rang rang gi gnas rnams la myi dga'-i bar gyurd to //de nas stong gsum gyi stong
4b1(mdo): chen po 'i /tshangs pa chen po de 'di snyam du /gang gis bdag rang gi gnas la dga' bar gyurd pa de lta bu sri snga ltas yin snyam sems so //de nas stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i tshangs pa des /stong gsum gyi 
4b2(mdo): stong chen po'i 'jig rten kyi khams thams cad dang ldan ba thams cad la bltas so //des stong gsum gyi stong chenapo 'i 'jig rten gyi khams de la bltas na /'di snyam du gang stong gsum gyi stong chen pi 'i 
4b3(mdo): 'jig rten gyi khams 'di yi byed pa po /dbang phyug bdag po dbang sgyur ba /de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom ba yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas de /do mad mya ngan las 'da' bar 'gyur te /gang g-is
4b4(mdo): bdag rang gi stan la 'dug par myi dga' ba dang /gang gis cho 'phrul bsam gyis myi khyab pa rmad du byung ba /'di lta bu dag snang bar gyurd pa 'di ni de yongs su mya ngan las 'da' ba'i snga 
4b5(mdo): ltas yin /'di ni de 'i mthu yin no snyam du sems ste /de myi dga' zhing yid dung zhes byed de /su zing zhes byed par gyurd nas /tshangs pa'i 'khor mang pos yongs su bskord 
4b6(mdo): cing mdund tu bdard te /myur ba myur bar rings par bcom ldan 'das ga la ba der song ngo //de 'i tshe stong gsum gyi stong chen po na tshangs pa lhag ma lus par gyurd pa dang /ma 'dus pa 
4b7(mdo): dang /tshangs pa de las gzhan ba ji snyed yod pa de dag 'phags pa'i chos la mngon par 'dul bar gyurd /'phags pa'i chos la gnas bar gyurd to //de nas stong gsum gyi stong chen pi 'i tshangs pa de 
4b8(mdo): bcom ldan 'das ga la bder song ste /bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la mgo bos phyag 'tshal nas bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsold to //bcom ldan 'das ji ltar gnas par bgyi ba dang /
4b9(mdo): ci ltar bsgrub par bgyi ba bdag la bka' stsald tu gsol /de skad ces gsold pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis tshangs pa chen po la 'd-i skad ces bka' stsald to /tshangs pa khyod nga ni tshangs pa /tshangs
5a1(mdo): //pa chen po 'i zild kyis gnon cing /zil kyis myi non pa /kun shes pa dang ldan ba brgya phrag bcu la dbang sgyur ba yin te /bdag gis sems can rnams byas /bdag gis sems can rnaMs sprulda
5a2(mdo): bdag gis 'jig rten byas /bdag gis 'jig rten spruld to zhes de snyam du sems shing de skad zer zhe na /bden nam /gsold pa /bcom ldan 'das mad do //bde bar gshegs pa mad do //bka' stsalda
5a3(mdo): pa /tshangs pa khyod sus byas /sus sprul /de nas tshangs pa chen po cang myi smra bar gyurd to //tshangs pa chen po de cang myi smra bar gyurd pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis 'di skad ces bka' stsald to //tshangs
5a4(mdo): pa gang gi tshe /bskald pas bsregs pa de 'i tshe /stong gsum ma gyi stong chen po 'i 'jig rten gyi khams 'di 'bar /rab tu 'bar /kun tu 'bar /kun tu rab tu mched cing 'bar te /mye lce gts-ig
5a5(mdo): tu gyurd pa'-i dus de dang /tshe de byung ba de tshangs pa khyod nyid kyis byas sam /khyod kyis spruld tam /gsold pa /bcom ldana'adas ma lags so //bka' stsald pa /tshangs pa gang
5a6(mdo): sa chen po 'd-i ni chu'i phung po la rten /chu yang rlung la rten /rlung yang nam mkha' la rtend te /'phang du dpag tshad drug khri brgyad stong yod cing myi 'jig pa de la ci snyam du sems /'di khyod kyis byas
5a7(mdo): shing khyod kyis spruld tam /gsoldad pa /bcom ldan 'das ma lags so //bde bar gshegs pa ma lags so /bka' stsald pa /tshangs gang nyi ma dang zla ba'i gzhal myed khang na /lha'i bu dag lham me ma
5a8(mdo): y-in /lhan ne ma yin zhing gzhal myed khang rnams stong par 'khod pa'i dus de dang /tshe de byung pa de la ji snyam du sems /tshangs pa de dag khyod kyis byas shing /khyod kyis spruld pa dang /khyod kyis byin kyis brlabs pa
5a9(mdo): yin nam /gsold pa /bcom ldan 'das mas lags so //bka' stsaladapa /gang sos ka dang /dbyar dang /ston dang /dgun dang /dgun smad dang /dpyid ka'i dus tshigs de dag la /khyod ci snyam du sems /

5b2(mdo): long gi dkyil 'khor ram /'bru mar dang /mar kyi snod dam /nor bu dang /shel rnams kyi nang ngam /de las gzhan ba'i snod dag pa rnams la /gzugs brnyan rnam pa sna tshogs snang ba 'di lta ste /shing ljon pa 
5b3(mdo): dang /nags tshal dang /shing ljon ba dang /nags tshal dang /kun dga' ra ba dang /bskyed mos tshal dang /gzhal myed khang snang ba 'am /grong ngam /grong khyer ram /grong rdal snang ba 'am /glang po che dang /
5b4(mdo): rta dang /rnga mo dang /ba lang dang /bong bu dang /lug dang /r-i dagsa dang /bya rnams snang ba 'am /zla ba dang /nyi ma dang /skar ma'-i tshogs snang ba 'am /nyan thos dang /rang sangs rgyas dang /byang

5b6(mdo): dang /myi ma yin ba snang ba de dag tshangs pa khyod kyis byas shing /khyod kyis spruld pa dang /khyod kyis byin gyis brlabs pa yin nam /gsold pa /bcom ldan 'das ma lags so //bka' stsald pa /tshangs 
5b7(mdo): pa /tshangs pa g.yang sa dang /r-i dang /ri 'i 'bab chu dang /rdzing rnams na dung dang /rnga bo che dang /rdza rnga dang /glu gar dang /ri dgas dang /bya dang /myi dang /myi ma yin ba rnams kyi sgra yi brag ca dang /gang de dang mthun

5b9(mdo): ba gang sems can rnams kyi rmyi lam na /gzugs sna tshogs mthong ba dang /sgra sna tshogs thos pa dang /dr-i sna tshogs snom ba dang /ro sna tshogs myong ba dang /reg bya sna tshogs reg pa dang /chos sna tshogs shes 
6a1(mdo): //pa dang /dga' ba dang /rtse ba sna tshogs myong ba dang /mchi ma byung ba dang /du ba dang /'dzogs pa dang /skrag pa dang /sdug bsngal ba mthong ba 'am /myong ba 'am /shes pa de dag tshangs pa khyod kyis

6a8(mdo): ba'i 'dzogs pa 'am /gzhan gnod pa byed pa'i 'jigs pa /sna tshogs de dag kyang tshangs pa khyo kyis byas shing /khyod kyis spruld pa dang /khyod kyis by-in ky-is brlabs pa yin nam //gsold pa /
6a9(mdo): bcom ldan 'das ma lags so //bka' stsald pa /tshangs pa gang sems can rnams gyi nad rnam pa sna tshogs rlung dang /mkhris pa dang /bad kan dang /'dus pa dang /dus dang /khams myi mthun ba las
6b1(mdo): gyurd pa 'am /gzhan gy-is gnod pa byas pa dang /sngon kyi las kyi sgrib pa las gyurd pa 'di lta ste /myig dang rna ba dang /sna dang lce dang /lus na ba dang /sems can rnams kyi yid la gdung ba'i sdug bsngal 
6b2(mdo): sna tshogs de dag khyod kyis byas shing /khyod kyis spruld pa dang /khyod kyis byin kyis brlabs pa yin nam /gsold pa /bcom ldan 'das ma lags so //bka' stsald pa /tshangs pa gang mu ge dang /
6b3(mdo): chu dang /'brog dang /sbrul dang /chom rkun dang /dgon pa dang /mtshon cha dang /nad dang /bar kyi bskald pa de dag tshangs pa khyod kyis byas shing /khyed kyis spruld pa dang /khyed kyis byin kyis brlabs yin 
6b4(mdo): nam /gsold pa /bcomaldan 'das ma lags so //bka' stsald pa /tshangs gang sems can rnams kyi 'bral ba'i sdug bsngal rnam pa sna tshogs pha ma dang /spun dang /sr-ing mo dang /
6b5(mdo): grogs po dang /nye du dang /snag gi gnyen mtshaMs dang /bu pho dang /chung ma dang /bu mo dag dang /bral ba'i sdug bsngal de dag khyod kyis byas shing /khyod kyis spruld pa dang /khyod kyis by-in
6b6(mdo): kyis brlabs pa yin nam /gsold pa /bcom ldana'adas ma lags so //bka' stsald pa /gang sems can rnams kyi sdug bsngal sna tshogs ya nga ba'i las la sbyor ba /skra 
6b7(mdo): dang /phabs dang /rgya skyegs dang /'khor lo 'tshong ba dang /rgya mtsho chen por 'gro ba dang /'brog dgon par 'jug pa'am /phyogs sna tshogs su skyabs 'bebs pa'am /zhing gi las rgya cher byed pa'am /
6b8(mdo): de las gzhan ba'i bya ba sna tshogs de dag kyang tshangs pa khyod kyis byas shing /khyod kyis spruld pa dang /khyod kyis byin kyis brlabs pa yin nam /gsold pa /bcom ldan 'das ma lags so //bka' stsald 
6b9(mdo): ba /tshangs pa gang sems can rnams kyi dge ba dang /myi dge ba'i las rnam pa sna tshogs yang dag par lend pa /sems can dmyal ba dang /dud 'gro'i skye gnas dang /gshin rje 'i 'jig rten dang /lha dang myi 
7a1(mdo): @#/ O /'i rgyu las byung ba rnams dang /gang sems can rnams kyi lus dang /ngag dang /yid kyis nyes par spyad pa dang /legs par spyad pa rnams sam /gang 'jig rten na myi dge ba bcu'i las kyi lam seMs 
7a2(mdo): can rnams la myi phan ba dang /sdug bsngal ba dang /gnod pa dang /ngan song ngan 'gro log par gyurd pa 'di lta ste /srog gcod pa dang /ma byin par lend pa dang /'dod pa la log par g.yem ba dang /brdzun 
7a3(mdo): tu smra ba dang /phra ma dang /ngag rtsub mo dang tshig khyal ba dang /brnab sems dang /gnod sems dang /log par lta ba de rnams kyang tshangs pa khyod kyis byas shing /khyod kyis spruld pa dang /khyod kyis byin kyis  
7a4(mdo): brlabs pa yin nam /gsold pa bcom ldan 'das ma la gas so //bka' stsald pa /tshangs pa gang sems can rnams kyi gnod byed kyi sdug bsngal 'di lta ste /mgo bcad pa 'am /lag pa
7a5(mdo): dang /rkang pa dang /rna ba dang sna bcad pa 'am /yan lag dang /nying lag thams cad bcad pa 'am /myer bsregs pa dang /'bru mar kyis gtord pa dang /srog dang bral ba dang /ral gyi dang mdung
7a6(mdo): thung dang /mtshon rtse gnyis pas btab pa dang /bsad pa dang /bcing ba dang /go rar gzhug pa dang /'thab pa dang /rtsod pa dang /'gyed pa de dag kyang tshangs pa khyod kyis  byas shing /khyod kyis skuld 
7a7(mdo): pa dang /khyod kyis byin gyis brlabs pa yin nam /gsald pa /bcom ldan 'das ma lags so //bka' stsald pa /tshangs pa gang sems chan rnams 'dod pa'i rgyu dang /'dod pa'i gzhis ma la log par spyod pa dang /
7a8(mdo): sring mo la log par spyod pa dang/tshul khrims dang ldan ba la log par spyod pa dang /sdig pa'i las thams cad la log par spyod pa de dag kyang /tshangs pa khyod kyis byas shing /khyod kyis sprul ba dang /khyod kyis byin gyi 
7a9(mdo): brlabs pa yin nam /gsold pa /bcom ldan 'dasam lags so //bka' suld pa /tshangs pa gang sems can rnams kyi gnod par byed pa /sna tshogs byad dang /ro langs dang /rigs sngags dang /sngags dang /
7b1(mdo): sman dang /'byung po dang /gdon 'bebs pa dang /gang de las gzhan ba dug sbyor ba dang /gang sems can rnams la gnod par gyurd pa dang /srog dang bral bar 'gyur ba dang /srog 'chad par 'gyur ba de dag kyang tshangs pa
7b2(mdo): khyod kyis byas shing /khyod kyis spruld pa dang /khyod kyis byin kyis brlabs pa y-in nam /gsold pa /bcom ldan 'das ma lags so //bka' stsald pa /gang rigs bzh-i 'i 'j-ig rten gy-i khams thams cad du spang
7b3(mdo): du my-i rung ba'-i 'jig rten kyi chos 'di lta ste /skye ba dang /rga ba dang /na ba dang /'chi ba dang /mya ngan dang /smre sngags 'don ba dang /sdug bsngal ba dang /yid myi bde ba dang /'khrug pa dang /my-i rtag pa'-i chos nyid
7b4(mdo): dang /zad pa'i chos nyid dang /'gyur ba'-i chos ny-id dang /mdza' ba dang /dga' ba thams cad dang tha dad du 'gyur ba dang /myed par 'gyur ba dang /bral bar 'gyur ba dang /bye bar 'gyurd pa de dag kyang /
7b5(mdo): tshangs pa khyod kyis byas shing /khyos kyis spruld pa dang /khyos kyis byin kyis brlabs pa yin nam /gsold pa /bcom ldan 'das ma lags /bka' stsald pa /tshangs pa gang seMs
7b6(mdo): can rnams kyi 'dod chags dang /zhe sdang dang /gt-i mug dang /sgrib pa dang /chod pa dang /bag la nyal ba dang /kun nas bkr-is pa dang /kun tu sbyor ba rnams dang /de las gzhan pa'i nyon mongs
7b7(mdo): pa rnam pa sna tshogs 'jug pa gang gis /sems can rnams chags par 'gyur ba dang /sdang bar 'gyur ba dang /rmongs par 'gyur ba dang /las mngon par 'du bya ba rnam pa sna tshogs mngon par 'du byed pa de dag kyang tshangs
7b8(mdo): pa khyod kyis byas shing /khyod kyis spruld pa dang /khyod kyis byin kyis brlabs pa y-in nam //gsold pa /bcom ldan 'das ma lags so //bka' stsald pa /tshangs gang 'di sems can dmyal ba dang /dud
7b9(mdo): 'gro'i skye gnas dang /gsh-in rje'-i 'jig rten dang /ngan 'gro rnam pa gsum po gang sems can rnams la gnod pa byed pa rnam pa sna tshogs kyis sdug bsngal sna tshogs myong ba de dag kyang tshangs pa khyod kyis byas shing /khyod
8a1(mdo): //ky-is sduld pa dang /khyod kyis byin gyis brlabs pa yin nam /gsold pa /bcom ldan 'das ma lags so //bka' stsald pa /tshangs pa gang 'di 'byung po 'i tshogs dang /sa bon gyi tshogs dang /
8a2(mdo): rtswa dang /sh-ing gel ba dang /sman dang /nags tshal chu na gnas pa dang /thang la gnas pa dang /me tog gi shing dang /'bras bu'-i sh-ing dang /spos kyi shing rnam pa sna tshogs sam /dngar ba dang /kha ba dang /lan tsha dang /
8a3(mdo): skyur ba dang /tsha ba dang /bska ba'-i ro 'i khyad par rnam pa sna tshogs yid du 'ong ba dang /y-id du my-i 'ong ba mngon par grub pa /gang sems can rnams la phan ba dang /gnod par 'gyur ba de la dza snyam du sems /tshangs
8a4(mdo): ba de dag khyod kyis byas sh-ing /khyod kyis spruld pa dang /khyod kyis
8a5(mdo): by-in ky-is brlabs pa y-in nam /gsold pa //bcom ldan 'das ma lags so //bka' stsald pa /tshangs pa gang 'gro ba lnga po
8a6(mdo): dag na yongs su 'khor ba /skye ba dang /'ch-i 'pho dang /mthon po dang /'byung ba dang /'j-ig pa 'd-i dag dang /tshangs pa gang sems can ma r-ig pa 'i sgrib pa dang /sred pa'i kun tu sbyor ba
8a7(mdo): dang ldan ba /kun tu rgyug c-ing 'khor ba de dag gi /sngon gyi mtha' my-i mngon pa dang /tshangs pa ma 'ongs pa'-i 'khor ba'-i lam rgyud ma chad pa /gang na lha dang bcas pa dang /tshangs pa dang bcas pa'i 'j-ig
8a8(mdo): rten 'd-i dang /dge sbyong dang /bram ze dang bcas pa'-i skye dgu thags 'khrugs pa lta bu /dru bu 'dzings pa lta bu 'ong ba dang /'gro bar gyurd pa 'jig rten 'di nas /'jig rten pha rol dang /'jig rten pha rol nas
8a9(mdo): 'jig rten 'dir kun tu rgyug cing 'khor ba /'khor ba las 'byung ba myi shes pa /de dag la ji snyam du sems /tshangs pa de dag khyod kyis byas shing /khyod kyis sprul pa dang /khyod kyis byin gy-is brlabs pa y-in
8a10(mdo): nam /gsold pa /bcom ldan 'das ma lags so /bde bar gshegs pa ma lags so //bka' stsald pa /'o na tshangs pa khyod ci 'i phyir 'di ltar ngas sems can 'di dag byas /ngas sprul /ngas by-in
8b1(mdo): ky-is brlabs /ngas 'j-ig rten byas /ngas sprul /ngas by-in gy-is brlabs snyam sems //gsold pa /bcom ldan 'das bdag n-i ma 'tshal ba /rtag tu lta bar gyurd pa ma spangs pas /phyind c-i log tu
8b2(mdo): gyurd pa lags te /bcom ldan 'das bdagani de bzhin gshegs pas gsungs pa'i chos 'dul ba rtag tu ma thos bas /sems can rnams bdag gis bgy-is /bdag gis sprul /'jig rten bdag gis bgy-is /
8b3(mdo): 'j-ig rten bdag g-is spruld ces bgy-i ba sdig pa can gy-i lta bar gyurd pa de lta bur gyurd to //bcom ldan 'das de bzh-in gshegs pa la don 'd-i nyid bdag zhu 'o //bcom ldan 'das 'j-ig rten 'd-i sus bgyis /'j-ig
8b4(mdo): rten 'd-i sus spruld /sems can rnams sus bgy-is /sus sprul /bcom ldan 'das 'd-i su'-i mthu lags /sus by-in ky-is brlabs /de skad ces gsold pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis tshangs pa
8b5(mdo): de la 'd-i skad ces bka' stsald to //tshangs pa 'j-ig rten n-i las ky-is byas /las ky-is spruld to //sems can rnams ni las ky-is byas /las kyis rgyu las byung ba /las kyis
8b6(mdo): rab tu phye'o //de ci 'i phyir zhe na /tshangs pa ma r-ig pa'-i rkyend kyis 'du byed rnams /'du byed kyi rkyend ky-is rnam par shes pa /rnam par shes pa'-i rkyend kyis my-ing dang gzugs /my-ing dang gzugs
8b7(mdo): kyi rkyen kyis skye mched drug /skye mched drug gi rkyen kyis reg pa /reg pa'-i rkyend ky-is tshor ba /tshor ba'-i rkyend kyis sred pa /sred pa'-i rkyend kyis lend pa /lend pa'-i rkyend kyis sr-id pa /sr-id pa'-i rkyend kyis
8b8(mdo): skye ba /skye ba'i rkyend kyis rga sh-i dang /mya ngan dang /smre sngags 'don ba dang /sdug bsngal ba dang /yid myi bde ba dang /'khrug pa rnams byung ste /de ltar sdug bsngal gyi phung po chen po 'di 'ba' sh-ig byung bar 'gyur
8b9(mdo): zh-ing /tshangs pa ma r-ig pa 'gag pa nas /sdug bsngal gyi phung po 'ba' sh-ig 'gag pa'-i bar du 'gyur te /tshangs pa las dang /chos kyi kun tu sbyor ba rnams yod na /sems can rnams 'byung bar snang ba dang /
9a1(mdo): //las dang chos kyi kun tu sbyor ba dang bral bas /'byung ba myed par snang ba ma rtogs par 'di la byed pa po 'am /byed du 'jug pa 'am /nye bar 'joms pa gang yang myed do //tshangs pa de ltar 'jig rten
9a2(mdo): 'di las zad pa dang /nyon mongs pa zad pa dang /sdug bsngal zad pa dang /sdug bsngal nye bar zhi bas zhi ba dang /nges par 'byung ba dang /rnam par zhi ba dang /yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa zhes bya'o //tshangs
9a3(mdo): pa de la /las gang yin ba de zad pa dang /nyon mongs gang yin ba de dang bral ba dang /sdug bsngal gang yin ba de rnam pa zhi ba dang /nub pa ma rtogs par yongs su mya ngan las 'dasapa zhes bya ba ci zhig yod de /
9a4(mdo): sangs rgyas rnams kyi mthu dang /sangs rgyas rnams kyi byin kyi brlabs kyis chos nyid de lta bu dag byung ngo //de ci'i phyir zhe na /tshangs pa sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams 'byung ba myed na /chos de
9a5(mdo): lta bu rab tu stond pa myi 'byung gi /tshangs pa gang gi tshe sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da'as rnams 'jig rten du byung ba de 'i tshe /zhi bar bya ba'i phyir khong du chud par dka' ba /zab pa /zab par
9a6(mdo): snang ba /chos rnam pa de lta bu dag yang dag par stond par 'gyur te /de thos pas sems can skye ba'i chos can rnams ni skye ba las yongs su grol bar 'gyur /sems can rga shi dang /mya
9a7(mdo): dang /smre sngags 'don ba dang /sdug bsngal ba dang /yid myi bde ba dang /'khrug pa'i chos can rnams ni 'khrug pa'i bar dag las yongs su grol bar gyur ro //tshangs pa de lta bas na 'di lta ste /'du byed thams cad ni
9a8(mdo): myig yor lta bu myi rtag pa /g.yo ba /myi brtan pa /ther zug ma yin ba /zad pa dang 'gyur ba'i chos can no zhes /tshangs pa sangs rgyas rnams kyis de bstand cing yang dag par bshad de /rnam par phye zhing gsal
9a9(mdo): bar mdzad de /'di ni sangs rgyas rnams kyi byin kyis rlabs so //sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa rnams kyi dam pa'i chos nub pa na yang 'di lta ste /'du byed tham cad myig yor lta bu'o
9b1(mdo): zhes bya ba de ny-id ky-i by-in ky-i rlabs y-in no //gal te de bzh-in gshegs pa rnams kyis 'du byed thams cad myig yor lta bur ma gz-igs su z-in na /de bzh-in gshegs pa rnam skad c-ig thang c-ig yud tsam yang 'du byed
9b2(mdo): thams cad myi lam dang /my-ig yor dang /brag cha lta bur myi stond pa zhig na /tshangs pa gang gi phyir de bzhin gshegs pa rnams 'du byed thams cad my-i lam dang /my-ig yor dag /brag cha lta bu dang /myi rtag pa dang /
9b3(mdo): g.yo ba dang /'gyur ba'i chos can du mkhyend pa de 'i phyir /de bzh-in gshegs pa nams 'du byed thams cad rmy-i lam dang /my-ig yoradang /brag cha lta bur stond to //de la mkhas pa de 'i mtshan ma mthong ba dag yod de /
9b4(mdo): de 'i mtshan ma mthong nas /rgyu de dang /rkyend de dang /dmyigs pa'i mtshan ma des //'du byed thams cad myi rtag par khong du chud cing /g.yo ba dang /'gyur ba'i cos can no zhes rnam par 'byed do //
9b5(mdo): 'du byed thams cad n-i 'j-ig c-ing 'gyur ro //dus dang /tsh-igs dang /dus tsh-igs kyis kyang 'jog go //skad c-ig dang /thang cig dang /phyod tsam dang /yud tsam dang /zhag dang /nyi ma dang /zla
9b6(mdo): ba dang /zla ba phyed dang /lo dang /lo brgya dang /bskal pa dang /bskald pa brgya dag kyang 'jig par 'gyur ro //mye'i phung po chen po yang 'bar nas shi bar 'gyur ro //chu'i phung po chen po yang bab nas myi
9b7(mdo): 'bab bo //rlung gi phung po chen po yang langs nas myi ldang ngo //sa'i phung po chen po yang byung nas myed par 'gyur ro //ri 'i phung po chen po 'khor yug dang /'khor yug chen po dang /lhun po dang /lhun po chen po dang /de
9b8(mdo): las gzhan ba'i ri nag po dag kyang byung nas myed par 'gyuraro //nyi ma dang /zla ba dang /skar ma'i tshogs ky-i 'khor dang bcas pa 'di dag byung nas /lha mer ma yin /lhan ner ma y-in /lhang nger ma yin zhing slar
9b9(mdo): lhung ngo //lha yul pa'i lha'i gzhal myed khang bzang po dag kyang 'jig par 'gyuraro //grong dang /grong khyer dang /grong rdal 'd-i dag dang /sh-ing ljon pa dang /nags tshal dang /bskyed mos tshal dang /kun dga' ra ba
10a1(mdo): //nyams dga' ba 'di dag kyang byung nas 'gag par 'gyur ro //lha dang myi 'di rnams kyang skye ba dang /rga ba dang /'pho ba dang /'byung bar 'gyur ro //skyes bu 'dzangs shing mkhas pa 'di dag kyang mtshan 
10a2(mdo): ma de mthong nas /kye ma'o /'du byed 'di thams cad ni myi rtag go /'dzag go /'du byed 'd-i thams cad myed par 'gro'o //'gyur ro /'du byed 'di thams cad zad par 'gyur ro /snyam nas skyo bar 'gyur ro //
10a3(mdo): skyes bu 'dzangs sh-ing mkhas pa 'di dag rgyu de dang /rkyend des khyim nas khyim myed par rab tu 'byung ngo //de dag dad pas khyim nas khyim myed par yang dag par rab tu byung nas kyang /'du byed thams cad myig yor lta bu 
10a4(mdo): dang /rmyi lam lta bu dang /brag cha lta bur rig ste /mtshan ma de dang /dmyigs pa de dang /rkyend des byang chub thob par 'gyur ro //chu 'i nang du zla ba'i dkyil 'khor dang /ny-i ma'-i dkyil 'khor snang ba dang /skar ma 
10a5(mdo): '-i gzugs snang ba de dag mthong nas /de bzh-in gshegs pas btams pa'am /de las gzhan ba'i dge ba'i bshes gnyen gyis gtams pa'am /bdag g-i blos brtags pas /'du byed thaMs
10a6(mdo): cad my-i lam dang /my-ig yor dang /brag cha lta bur rtogs nas dad pas khyim nas khyims myed par rab tu byung ste /rgyund tu zhugs pa'-i 'bras bu dang /lan cig phy-ir 'ong ba'i 'bras bu dang /phyir myi 
10a7(mdo): 'ong ba'i 'bras bu thob pa dang /dgra bcom ba nyid mngon gsum du byed pa dang /gang zag byang chub sems dpa'-i theg pa pa bzod pa dang po thob pa dang /bzod pa gny-is pa dang /gsum pa thob pa dang /bla na myed pa 
10a8(mdo): yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub thob par 'gyur ba'i skyes bu mkhas pa dag yodo /sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das mya ngan las 'das pa rnams kyi chos bstand pa 'di nyid 'jig rten du 'byung ste /de thos pas sems can 
10a9(mdo): rnams nyan thos kyi theg pa dang /rang sangs rgyas kyi theg pa dang /theg pa chen po bla na myed pa rnam pa thams cad kyi mchog dang ldan ba dang /theg pa gsum po dag gis nges par 'byung bar 'gyur ro //tshangs pa 'di 
10b1(mdo): ltar 'di yang sangs rgyas rnams kyi byin kyi rlabs su rig par bya ste /tshangs pa gang gis skyes bu mkhas pa dag gis 'du byed 'di dag ni sdug bsngal ba /g.yo ba myi brtan pa /zad pa dang /'gyur ba'i chos can te /my-i
10b2(mdo): lam dang /myig yor dang /brag cha lta bu 'o //zhes bya ba'i mtshan ma de mthong nas yang dag par skyo bar 'gyur ba 'di ni /sangs rgyas rnaMs kyi yul dang /sangs rgyas kyi byin kyi rlabs so //sngon spyad cing sngon gyi rgyu
10b3(mdo): las 'byung ba'-i sems can sngon yongs su smy-ind par byas pa' dag gis chos kyi sgra 'di lta bu thos nas /'du byed 'di dag thams cad ni myi rtag pa 'du byed rnams ni 'jig pa ste /'du byed thams cad ni
10b4(mdo): my-i lam dang /brag cha lta bu'o zhes de bzhin gshegs pa la mos par 'gyur /mngon par dad par 'gyur ro //sems can gang dag gis sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi drung du tshangs par spyod pa
10b5(mdo): spyad par 'gyur ba'am /khyim par gyurd pas bslab pa'-i gzh-i rnams yongs su bzung bar gyurd pa de dag kyang /rgyu de dang /rkyend des 'd-i ltar 'du byed rnams myi rtag pa dang /'du byed
10b6(mdo): rnams sdug bsngal ba dang /'du byed rnams 'jig pa dang /'du byed rnams my-i lam dang /myig yor dang /brag cha lta bu 'o zhes 'du byed rnams de lta bu'i tshul du rtogs par 'gyur te /rgyu de dang /
10b7(mdo): rkyend des dad pas khy-im nas khyim myed par rab tu byung nas /sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams 'j-ig rten du ma byung yang /sangs rgyas ky-i byin kyi rlabs nyid dang /sangs rgyas la dge ba'-i rtsa ba bskyed pa'-i
10b8(mdo): rkyend kyis byang chub thob par 'gyur ro //tshangs pa rnam grangs des kyang /'di ltar 'di ni sangs rgyas kyi byin ky-i rlabs dang /'di ni sangs rgyas kyi yul y-in bar r-ig par bya'o //tshangs pa stong gsum gyi stong chen
10b9(mdo): po'-i 'j-ig rten gy-i khams 'd-i yang sangs rgyas kyi /sangs rgyas kyi zhing y-in te /tshangs pa 'di ni nga sngon byang chub sems dpa'i spyad pa spyod pa na /bskald pa bye ba khrag khr-ig brgya stong grangs myed par /sangs
11a1(mdo): //rgyas bcom ldan 'das tshad myed /grangs myed pa rnams kyi drung du /dge ba'i rtsa ba tshad myed grangs myed pa bskyed de /tshul khrims dang /dka' thub dang /tshangs par spyod pa dang /de las gzhan ba'-i dka'
11a2(mdo): ba spyad pa bye ba khrag khr-ig brgyad stong dag g-is ji ltar dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad 'phel bar 'gyur ba dang /j-i ltar yongs su dag par 'gyur ba dang /dus dang /tshod dang /snod ji lta ba bzh-in du 'dul ba nye bar bzung ste /
11a3(mdo): sangs rgyas ky-i zhing yongs su bzung ngo //yongs su sbyangs so /ngas sbyin ba dang /snyan par smra ba dang /don spyad pa dang /don mthun ba bsdu ba'i dngos po bzhi po dag gis sems can rnams yang dag par bsdus te de
11a4(mdo): dag nga'i smon lam ny-id kyis /sangs rgyas ky-i zh-ing 'd-ir skyes nas ngas chos bstand pa yang shes so //nga '-i chos bstand pas 'dul bar yang 'gyur te /brgya byin rnams kyi ma yin /tshangs pa
11a5(mdo): rnams kyi ma y-in /'j-ig rten skyong ba rnams ky-i ma y-in no //tshangs pa rnams kyis kyang de 'di ltar sangs rgyas ky-i zhing 'd-i ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ky-i y-in kyi /brgya by-in rnams ky-i
11a6(mdo): ma yin /tshangs pa rnams ky-i ma yin /'j-ig rten skyong ba rnas ky-i ma yin /gzhan mu stegs spyod ba can dang /kun tu rgyu ba rnams kyis ma y-in bar r-ig par bya'o //de nas my-i mched ky-i bdag po tshangs
11a7(mdo): pa 'bum phrag bcu'-i bdag po tshangs pa de dang /de las gzhan ba'-i tshangs pa rnams n-i skyo zhing /y-id byung bar gyurd te /tsh-ig 'd-i skad ces ci tsam du sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da'as rnams chos rgya chen po dang ldan ba
11a8(mdo): ngo mtshar to /ci tsam du sangs rgyas bcomaldan 'das rnams rmad du byung ba'-i chos dang ldan ba ngo mtshard to /zhes smras so /de nas stong gsum gyi stong chen po'-i tshangs pa de /de bzh-in gshegs pa dgra bcom ba yang
11a9(mdo): dag par rdzogs pa'-i sangs rgyas la rmad du 'dzind par gyurd te /ci tsam du sangs rgyas bcam ldan 'das rnams kyi yul bsam gy-is my-i khyab cing mtha' yas pa ngo mtshard to snyam mo //de nas stong gsum gyi ste ngo chen
11b1(mdo): po'-i tshangs pa chen po de /bcom ldan 'das kyi slob mar khas blangs nas /bcom ldan 'das la skyabs su song ste /bcom ldan 'das n-i bdag gi stond pa /bde bar gshegs pa ni bdag g-i stond pa lags na /
11b2(mdo): bcom ldan 'das j-i ltar gnas par bgy-i ba dang /j-i ltar bsgrub par bgy-is pa bdag la bka' stsald tu gsol zhes /bcom ldan 'das la/bka' lung yang nod par gyurd to //de skad ces gsold pa dang /
11b3(mdo): bcom ldan 'das ky-is tshangs pa chen po la 'd-i skad ces bka' stsald to //tshangs pa stong gsum gy-i stong chen po'i 'j-ig rten gi khams 'd-i ni nga y-i sangs rgyas ky-i sangs rgyas ky-i zhing yin te /'d-i ni nga '-i sangs rgyas
11b4(mdo): kyi zhing y-in te /tshangs pa stong gsum gyi stong chen po 'i 'jig rten gyi khams 'd-i khyod la gtad kyis /khyod kyis c-i nas kyang /nga '-i rjes su 'jug par gy-is la /dge ba'-i lam 'd-i dang /dge ba'-i tshul 'd-i dang /bla
11b5(mdo): na myed pa'-i sangs rgyas kyi tshul dang chos kyi tshul dang /dge 'dun gyi tshul 'd-i rgyun my-i 'chad par gy-is sh-ig //nga n-i 'd-i '-i skyes bu tha ma'o //tshangs pa bu'-i thu bo gzhon nu thugs las skyes pa /
11b6(mdo): zhal nas skyes pa /chos las byung ba /chos ky-is spruld pa /byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po byams pa snying rje chen po dang ldan ba /skye bo mang po'i don 'dod pa /phan ba 'dod
11b7(mdo): pa /bde ba 'dod pa /grub pa dang /bde ba 'dod pa 'ong bar 'gyur te /de 'ang stong gsum gy-i stong chen po '-i 'j-ig rten gy-i khams 'd-i ci ltar ngas gnas pa bzh-in du chos kyis gnas par 'gyur gyis /c-i ltar
11b8(mdo): nga la rjes su mthun par byed pa de bzh-in du de la rjes su mthun par gy-is shig /ci nas kyang khyod kyis dge ba'-i lam 'd-i dang /dge ba'-i tshul 'd-i dang /sangs rgyas kyi tshul dang /chos kyi tshul dang /dge 'dun
11b9(mdo): kyi tshul 'd-i rgyun my-i 'chad par gyis sh-ig //de c-i '-i phy-ir zhe na /tshangs pa c-i tsam du dge ba'-i tshul 'd-i lta bu 'd-i rgyun my-i 'chad pa de tsam du /sangs rgyas ky-i tshul dang /chos kyi tshuld dang /dge 'dun gyi tshul rgyun
12a1(mdo): //'chad par my-i 'gyur /brgya byin dang /tshangs pa dang /'jig rten skyong ba dang /lha dang myi 'i tshul dang /thard pa'i tshul nas /mya ngan las 'da' ba'-i tshul kyi bar du rgyun chad par myi 'gyur ro //
12a2(mdo): tshangs pa de lta bas na /stong gsum gyi stong chen po 'i 'jig rten kyi khams sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'di khyod la gtad do //tshangs pa khyod la bka' stsald pa n-i 'di yin no //rjes su bstand pa ni 'di yin gyis khyod
12a3(mdo): kyis 'd-i bzhin du gyis sh-ig //nga ni 'di yi skyes bu tha ma'o /de 'i tshe stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams na /tshangs pa dang /tshangs pa chen po ji snyed 'khod pa de dag thams cad /'phags pa'-i
12a4(mdo): chos la mngon par 'dul bar gyurd te /tshangs pa chen po de gc-ig pu ni ma gtogs so //tshangs pa chen po stong gi tshangs pa de yang bcom ldan 'das kyis /'phags pa chos la btul zh-ing bzhag par
12a5(mdo): gyurd to ///tshangs pa'-i le'u ste /dang po rdzogs s+ho ///de '-i tshe bdud ky-i bu de dpon zhes bya ba /bcom ldan 'das la dad c-ing dang ba chen po
12a6(mdo): thob par gyurd pa des /bcom ldan 'das yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa'-i sgra thos nas /sdug bsngal zh-ing y-id my-i bde ba ste /my-i dga' zh-ing sdug bsngal ba dang /y-id myi bde ba chen po
12a7(mdo): dang ldan bar gyurd /skyo zhing yid byung la /spu z-ing zhes byed par gyurd nas /myur ba myur bar rings pa'-i tshul du /bcom ldan 'das ga la ba der song ste /phy-in nas /bcom ldan 'das ky-i zhabs la mgo bos phyag
12a8(mdo): 'tshal te /phyogs gcig tu 'dug go //phyogs gcig tu 'dug nas /bdud kyi bu ded dpon gyis bcom ldan 'das la 'd-i skad ces gsold to /bcom ldan 'das bdag gsol ba 'tshal na /sman par bgyi ba'i
12a9(mdo): slad du yongs su mya ngan las myi 'da' bar gsol //bcom ldan 'das lha dang bcas pa'i 'jig rten la brtse ba nye bar bzung ste /skye bo mang po la sman pa dang /skye bo mang po la bde ba dang /'j-ig rten la thugs
12b1(mdo): brtse ba dang /lha dang my-i rnams ky-i don dang /sman pa dang bde ba'i slad du bzhugs su gsol //bcom ldan 'das 'j-ig rten ha cang myur du long ba dang /myig ma mchis pa dang /ston pa ma mchis pa dang /'drend pa ma mch-is
12b2(mdo): pa dang /dpung gnyen ma mchis pa dang /skyabs ma mch-is pa dang /rten ma mch-is par myi 'gyur bar mdzad du gsol /de skad ces gsold pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis bdud kyi bu ded dpon la 'd-i skad ces bka'
12b3(mdo): stsald to //ded dpon khyod kyi pha bdud sdig can gyis /nga la bcom ldan 'das yongs su mya ngan las 'da' bar gsol //bde bar gshegs pa yongs su mya ngan las 'das bar gsol //bcom ldan 'da'as khyod deng
12b4(mdo): yongs su mya ngan las 'ad ba'i dus la bab bo zhes gsol ba btab nas /khyod kyi pha bdud sdig cad la nga yongs su mya ngan las 'da'o zhes smras ste /ded dpon bdud sdig can la ngas yongs su mya
12b5(mdo): ngan las 'da' bar bya ba'-i skabs phye'o //ded dpon bdud sdig cad de la ngas khas blangs pa de bya ba'-i dus la 'ang bab po //de skad ces bka' stsald pa dang /bdud kyi bu ded dpon gy-is bcom
12b6(mdo): ldan 'das la 'd-i skad ces gsold to //bcom ldan 'das bdud sdig can de ni pha ma lags kyi /bcom ldan 'das de ni bdag g-i dgra lags gshed po slar rgol ba lags shing /bcom ldan 'das
12b7(mdo): bdag dang myi mdza' ba lags te /gnod par 'tshal ba /myi sman bar 'tshal ba lags so //bcom ldan 'das sdig can de ni bdag la grub pa dang bde ba ma lags par 'tshal ba lags so /bcom ldan 'das gang shes
12b8(mdo): rab kyi sgron ma 'd-i lta bu dang /shes rab kyi snang ba chen po dang /ye shes kyi snang ba'-i sgron ma chen po 'di lta bu yongs su mya ngan las 'da' bar 'tshal ba'i sdig can de n-i sdig pa'-i bsam ba can /sdig pa'i sbyor ba can de /
12b9(mdo): lha dang /myi dang lha ma y-in du bcas pa'i 'j-ig rten sun 'by-in pa' dgra lags so //bcom ldana'adas lha dang bcas pa'i 'j-ig rten sems can sdig pa chen po 'jig rten du byung ngo zhes gang yang dag par smra bas smra ba de
13a1(mdo): @#//n-i /bcom ldan 'das bdud lags so zhes yang dag par smra bas smra bar bgyi'o //bcom ldan 'das gang bdag la myi phan bar zhugs pa dang /gzhan la myi phan par zhugs pa dang /skye bo mang
13a2(mdo): po la myi phan bar zhugs pa zhes gang yang dag par smra bas smra par bgy-i'o /lha dang bcas /bdud dang bcas /tshangs pa dang bcas pa'i 'jig rten dge sbyong dang /bram zer bcas pa'-i skye dgu la /gnod pa dang
13a3(mdo): my-i phan ba dang /sdug bsngal ba dang /grub pa dang /bde ba ma lags ba dang /log par ltung ba'i phyir zhugs pa zhes bgyi ba n-i /bcom ldan 'das bdud lags so /zhes yang dag par smra bas smra bar bgyi'o /
13a4(mdo): chos dang ldan ba dam bcas pa dang /chos dang myi ldan ba '-i dam bcas pa /bcom ldan 'das bdag gis mngon gsum du thos shing /mngon sum du bzung nas /bcom ldan 'das gang bdud sdig can
13a5(mdo): gy-is dam bcas pa de n-i /chos dang my-i ldan ba'i dam bcas pa lags so //bcom ldan 'das khyod ni dam bcas pa 'di la brtan zh-ing snying por 'du shes pa lags na /bcom ldan 'das
13a6(mdo): skye bo mang po la sman pa dang /skye bo mang po la bde ba dang /'jig rten la thugs brtse ba dang /lha dang my-i rnams la phan ba dang bde ba'-i slad du /bskald par bzhugs su gsol /bcom ldan
13a7(mdo): 'das yun r-ing du bzhugs na /de ni skye bo mang po la sman pa dang /skye bo mang po la bde ba dang /lha dang my-i rnams la bde bar gyurd te /de 'i slad du bcom ldan 'das /lha dang bcas pa'i 'jig rten la thugs
13a8(mdo): brtse ba'-i nye bar bzung ste /myur du yongs su mya ngan las my-i 'da' bar gsol //de skad ces gsold pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis bdud kyi bu ded dpon la 'd-i skad ces bka' bstsald to //ded dpon legs
13a9(mdo): so /legs so //ded dpon khyod skye bo mang po'i don 'dod pa dang /skye bo mang po la phan ba dang /bde ba dang /grub pa dang /bde bar 'dod pas de ltar bya'o //ded dpon gang dag rgyal po rgyal rigs spyi bo nas
13b1(mdo): dbang bskur ba la bsnyen bkur bar byed cing /phan pa dang /bde bar khyab par byed pa dang /rgyal po rgyal rigs spyi bo nas dbang bskur ba'-i yul 'khor dang /grong rdal dang /ljongs rnams dang /gzhon nu rnams dang /
13b2(mdo): blon po rnams la rtag tu rgyun my-i 'chad par bsnyen bkur dang /r-im gro byed pa la zhugs par gyurd pa de dag ni ded dpon rgyal rigs spyi bo nas dbad bskur ba las /nor mang po dang longs spyod chen po thob cing /rgyal 
13b3(mdo): po rgyal r-igs de lan tu phan chen po 'dogs te /bu dang chung mar bcas /grogs po dang /nye du dang /snag gi mnyen 'tshams su bcas pa la phan bas khyab par byed do //ded dpon de bzhin du gang khyod de bzh-in
13b4(mdo): gshegs pa dgra bcom ba yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas chos kyi rgyal bo bla na myed pa la sems dang ba'i dag pa'-i brtsam ba 'di lta bu 'di dbul na /ded dpon de bzhin gshegs pa la sems dang bas bsnyen
13b5(mdo): bkur ba dang /r-im 'gror bya ba'i phyir khyod la dbugs dbyung ba sbyin no //khyod la dge ba'-i rtsa ba rgya chen po 'i longs spyod chen po sbyin no //ded dpon khyod /de bzh-in gshegs pa la sems
13b6(mdo): dang ba'-i dge ba'-i rtsa ba bskyed na dbugs 'byung ngo //ded dpon khyod dge ba'i rtsa ba 'di dang /dad pa dang /dang ba 'd-is /nga mya ngan las 'das nas ma 'ongs pa'-i dus na /rang sangs rgyas
13b7(mdo): snying rje can zhes bya bar 'gyur ba'i dus de lta bu dang /tshe de lta bu yod kyis shes par gyis sh-ig //ded dpon nga 'das nas dam pa'i chos thams cad kyi thams cad du nub pa na /bdud sdig can rab tu mgu bar 'gyur
13b8(mdo): ba dang /dga' ba dang /rangs par 'gyur ba'-i dus de lta bu dang /tshe da lta bu yod par 'gyur ro //de shi nad tu dga' zh-ing mgu ba las /bdud kyi gnas nas 'chi 'pho bar 'gyur ro //de bdud kyi gnas nas 'ch-i
13b9(mdo): 'phos ma thag tu mnar myed pa'i sems can dmyal ba chen por lus thob par 'gyur te /der yang de mnar myed pa'i sdug bsngal rnam pa sna tshogs myong bar 'gyur ro //de ci 'i phyir zhe na /ded dpon 'di ltar de
14a1(mdo): //n-i/shes rab ky-i sgron ma chen po /shes rab kyi snang ba chen po 'di lta bu myed par 'gyur ba la dga' ba'i phyir ro //ded dpon gang bdag gsod pa dang /bdag la gnod pa dang /bdag myi grub par
14a2(mdo): zhugs pa zhes yang dag par smra bas smra ba de ni /bdud yin no zhes yang dag par smra ba smra bar bya'o //ded dpon de ci 'i phyir zhe na /'di ltar nga yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa'i dam pa'i chos ci srid du gnas pa
14a3(mdo): de srid du bdud sdig can /bdud ky-i gnas na 'dug go //ded dpon nga'i dam pa'i chos nub ma thag tu de nas bdud sdig can rangs pa chen po skye bar 'gyur zhing tsh-im ba chen po dang /dga' ba chen po dang /mchog tu
14a4(mdo): dga' ba chen po dang /mgu ba chen po dang /y-id bde ba chen po skye bar 'gyur te /de skad cig de nyid dang /thang cig de nyid dang /yud tsam de nyid la bdud kyi gnas nas sh-i 'phos te /sems
14a5(mdo): can dmyal ba chen po mtshams myed par ltung bar 'gyur ro //ded dpon 'di lta ste dper na /my-i la la zhig sh-ing sa la ljon pa'-i me tog dang /'bras bu mang po dang ldan ba chen po la
14a6(mdo): 'dzegs nas /des shing sa la ljon pa de las me tog dang /'bras bu y-id du 'ong ba dag spyad de /yal ga gang na gnas pa'i yal ga de ny-id bcag na /ded dpon de ji snyam du sems /my-i de yal ga
14a7(mdo): chag pa de la gnas su rung ngam /gang yal ga gang la 'dzegs te /bde bar 'dug pa'i yal ga de ny-id gcog pa'i myi de yang ci mkhas pa'i rang bzh-in can y-in nam /gsold pa bcom ldan 'das ma lags
14a8(mdo): so //bde bar gshegs pa ma lags so //bka' stsald pa /ded dpon bdud sdig can gang de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom ba yang dag par rdzogs pa'-i sangs rgyas yongs su mya ngan las 'da' bar 'dod ci
14a9(mdo): de bzhin gshegs pas gsungs pa'i chos 'dul bar nub par dga' ba yang de ltar snang bar ngas bsams so //de c-i '-i phy-ir zhe na /'d-i ltar nga '-i dam pa'-i chos ci srid du gnas pa de sr-id du de bdud ky-i gnas na
14b1(mdo): 'dug go //nga y-i chos nub bar gyurd na bdud sdig can de dga' ba bskyed de /mgu rangs sh-ing y-id bde ba bskyed de /de de ma thag tu bdud ky-i gnas nas 'ch-i 'phos nas sems can dmyal ba chen po mnar myed par
14b2(mdo): rab tu ltung bar 'gyur te /ded dpon 'd-i lta ste dper na /my-i de sh-ing sa la ljon ba de la bdag la gnod pa dang /gzhan la gnod pa ltar /bdud sdig can yang de lta bu y-in bar mthong ngo //ded dpon bdud sdig can ni
14b3(mdo): bdag la gnod pa'i phy-ir zhugs pa y-in no //ded dpon bdud sd-ig can n-i gzhan la gnod pa'-i phy-ir zhugs pa ste /ded dpon bdud sd-ig canade de '-i tshe de 'i dus na /sems can dmyal ba chen mo mnar myed par ltung
14b4(mdo): nas /sdug bsngal gyi tshor ba my-i bzad pa tsha ba srog 'phrog pa myong nas 'chi ste /'tsha kar tshor ba myong nas nga rjes su dran bar 'gyur te /bcom ldan 'das de bzh-in gshegs pa dgra bcom ba yang dag par rdzogs
14b5(mdo): pa'-i sangs rgyas bden bar gsung ba /gzhan du my-i gsung ba /log par myi gsung ba des /lus bsdams pa legs /ngag bsdams pa legs /y-id bsdams pa legs te /lus
14b6(mdo): ky-is legs par spyad pa dang /ngag g-is legs par spyad pa dang/y-id kyis legs par spyad pa'-i rnam par smy-ind pa n-i /sdug pa dang /'dod pa dang /dga' ba dang /y-id du 'ong ba
14b7(mdo): mngon par grub po //lus ky-is nyes par spyad pa dang /ngagag-is nyes par spyad pa dang/y-idaky-is nyes par spyad pa'-i rnam parasmy-ind pa n-i /my-i sdug pa dang /my-i 'dod pa dang /my-i dga' ba dang /y-id du
14b8(mdo): 'ong ba mngon par 'grub po zhes gsungs pa de legs par gsungs pa'o //bdag ni sngon gyi lus kyis nyes par spyad pa dang ldan bar gyurd /ngag gis nyes par spyad pa dang y-id ky-is nyes par spyad pa dag
14b9(mdo): ldan bar gyurd te /bdag las de 'i rnam par smy-ind pas /da ltar sems can dmyal ba chen po 'd-ir skyes nas /nga 'd-i lta bu'-i sdug bsngal my-i bzad pa /drag pa tsha ba y-id du my-i 'ong ba /'ch-i ba'-i mthar
15a1(mdo): //'gyur ba /srog 'phrog pa'-i tshor ba myong bar gyurd to snyam mo //de de 'i tshe tshangs pas legs par smras pa thos nas /sems dang bar gyurd te /de sems dang bar gyurd nas sems can dmyal ba chen 
15a2(mdo): po nas sh-i 'phos te /sum cu rtsa gsum gy-i lha rnams dang skal ba mnyam bar skye bar 'gyur ro //de ci 'i phyir zhe na /ded dpon de n-i 'd-i lta bu y-in te /gang dag de bzh-in gshegs pa la nyes pa 'i bar du gcod pas 
15a3(mdo): kun tu spyod pa de dag ni de bzh-in gshegs pa la nyas pa'i bar du gcod pas kun tu spyad pas lus zhig nas /sems can dmyal ba chen por skye bar 'gyur ro //ded dpon gang dag de bzhin gshegs pa la byams pa 
15a4(mdo): kun tu spyod kyi /nyes par ma yin ba de dag ni lus zhig nas /bde 'gro mtho ris kyi 'jig rten du lha'-i nang du skye bar 'gyurd te /de dag dge ba'-i rtsa ba des sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ky-i chos nyams su blangs
15a5(mdo): nas de ny-id du dge ba'-i rtsa ba bskyed de /r-im gyis nye bar lend pa myed par yongs su mya ngan las 'da'o //de bas na ded dpon khyod kyis de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom ba yang dag par rdzog+sa 
15a6(mdo): pa'i sangs rgyas la /sems dang bas byas pas /ded dpon khyod dge ba'-i rtsa ba de dang /rgyu de dang /rkyend des gang gi tshe byaMs pa 'j-ig rten du byung ba de 'i tshe /ded dpon khyod de '-i tshe /de '-i dus
15a7(mdo): na de bzh-in gshegs pa byams pa'-i gsung rab de la 'byung ngo //byung nas kyang sems can gnyid kyis log pa dang /myos pa dang /rab tu myos pa rnams la /kye sems can khyed longs la sbyor bar gyis /'bad par 
15a8(mdo): gyis /rtsol bar gy-is shig /de bzh-in gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas 'j-ig rten du 'byung ba rnyed par dka' ste /'d-i lta ste dper na /shing u dum ba ra 'i me tog brgya la brgya 
15a9(mdo): lam na /'byung bar 'gyur ba de bzh-in du /de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyang brgya la brgya lam nas /'j-ig rten du 'byung bar 'gyur ro //gnas myed pa zhi bar 'gyur ba yongs su mya ngan las 'da' bar 'gyur 
15b1(mdo): ba yang dag par rdzogs pa'-i sangs rgyas ky-is bstand pa'-i chos stond pa yang brgya la brgya lam na 'o //myi 'i lus thob cing 'phags pa'-i gnas na tshul bzhin du gnas pa yang brgya la brgya la ma na snang ngo /
15b2(mdo): my-i khom ba brgyad spangs de lta bu'i dal ba phun sum tshogs pa rnyed pa yang brgya lam brgya lam na ste /bag yod par legs par brtson par gy-is sh-ig /phy-is 'gyod par ma gyurd c-ig ces bskul bar 'gyur ro //
15b3(mdo): ded dpon khyod de ltar de bzh-in gshegs pa byams pa'-i gsung rab dang /de bzh-in gshegs pa byamasapa'i chos kyi rgyal po'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing du /de 'i gsung gi tshig rjes su 'dzind cing rjes su skyong bar 'gyur ro /
15b4(mdo): byams pa'i sems 'khon myed pa dang /'gran zla myed pa dang /kha na ma tho ba myed pa dang /phan pa dang /bde bas khyab par byed par 'gyur ro //ded dpon khyod dge ba'i rtsa ba des bdud sdig can
15b5(mdo): 'das pa'-i 'og tu bdud kyi gnas na /dbang phyug chen po 'i bdag po 'i bdud byed par 'gyur ro //ded dpon sems can gang dag de bzh-in gshegs pa la /tha na sems bskyed pa gcig tsam
15b6(mdo): gy-i dge ba'-i rtsa ba bskyed par 'gyur ba de dag thams cad n-i /ded dpon dge ba'i rtsa ba des my-i 'ch-i bar nye bar 'gro //my-i 'ch-i ba la gzhol /my-i 'ch-i ba'-i mthar 'gyur ro //ded dponakhyod ni dge ba

20b6(mdo): nges par yongs su mya ngan las 'da'i dgos so //sgra gcan zin sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da'as rnams la shind tu rnam par zh-i ba ma rtogs par chos nyid gzhan myed /gnas gzhan myed
20b7(mdo): do //sgra gcan z-in shind tu rnam par zhi ba zhes bya ba'-i ni /shind tu zhi ba /shind tu bsil bar gyurd pa /sh-ind tu mthar thug pa /sh-ind tu bde ba shind tu grub pa dang bde ba ste /'di lta ste /gzhi
20b8(mdo): myed pa /mya ngan las 'das pa'i dbyings so /sgra gcan zin skye ba sdug bsngal ba /rga ba sdug bsngal /na ba sdug bsngal /'ch-i ba sdug bsngal /yang dang yang mdza' ba dang /'bral ba sdug bsngal
20b9(mdo): my-i mdza' ba dang 'grogs pa sdug bsngal /phung po'i khur thogs pa sdug bsngal lo //sgra gcan zin gang dag yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa de dag n-i bde ba yin gyis /sgra gcan zin khyod kyang yongasu
21a1(mdo): //mya ngan las 'da' bar gyis shig //bcom ldan 'das de bzh-in gshegs pa shag kya thub pa de yang yongs su mya ngan las 'da' bar 'gyur te /gang na skye ba myed pa /rga ba myed /na ba myed /
21a2(mdo): 'chi ba myed /mdza' ba dang bral ba myed /myi mdza' ba dang phrad ba myed /sdug bsngal ba dang /y-id my-i bde ba myed pa der khyed gnyi ga 'dong bar 'gyur gyis /sgra gcan z-in khyod myi dgar ma byed /mya ngan ma byed /
21a3(mdo): smre sngags ma 'dond c-ig /sgra gcan zin chos nyid rjes su dran bar gy-is sh-ig /yang dag pa ma y-in ba phy-ind ci log tu 'dz-ind pa ma gtogs par 'di la skye ba gang yin /'di la rga ba gang y-in /'di la na ba
21a4(mdo): gang yin /'di la 'ch-i ba gang yin /'pho ba gang yin /'byung ba gang yin /sgra gcan zin byis ba so so 'i skye bo thos pa dang my-i ldan ba 'phags pa'i chos rnams ma mthong ba /'phags
21a5(mdo): pa'i chos rnams ma bslabs pa /'phags pa'i chos rnams la myi mkhas pa /'phags pa'i chos rnas la myi gnas pa /'phags pa'i chos rna mas ky-is ma dul ba /'phags pa
21a6(mdo): '-i chos rnams ma shes pa'i phyir /'du shes phy-ind ci log dang /lta ba phyin ci log tu phyind ci log pas /skye ba dang /rga ba dang /na ba dang /'chi ba dang /'pho ba dang /'byung ba dang /kun tu rgyug pa
21a7(mdo): dang /'khor ba dang /rnam par skam ba dang /rgud pa dang /my-i dga' ba dang /mya ngan dang /smre sngags 'don ba dang /brang brdung bar 'gyur ro //sgra gcan zin 'phags pa'i chos 'dul ba 'di ni 'du
21a8(mdo): byed thams cad rnam par zh-i bas phy-is gong du bya ba ci yang myed do //sgra gcan z-in stond pa'-i nyan thos /bcom ldan 'das kyi sras kyis gang bya ba de khyod kyis byas zind to //sgra gcan z-ind stond pa bcom
21a9(mdo): ldan 'das kyis kyang /nyan thos rnams kyi bya ba gang yin ba de mdzad zand te /da gong du mdzad pa myed kyis /sgra gcan z-in khyod da mya ngan ma byed /myi dgar ma byed //smre sngags ma 'don par /sgra gcan
21b1(mdo): z-in khyod bcom ldan 'das shag kya thub pa /shag kya'-i rgyal po chen po de la tha mar blta ba dang /phyag bya ba dang /bsnyen bkur bya ba'i phyir song shig /phyis 'gyod par gyurd ta re /sgra gcan z-in de bzhin gsheg+sa
21b2(mdo): pa shag kya thub pa de /de ltar gyad rnams nye bar gnas pa /shing sa la 'i tshal na seng ge ltar glo g.yas pas mnal pa mdzad de /khyod kyang gz-igs par bzhed kyis /sgra gcan z-in khyod nges par 'dug rgos so /
21b3(mdo): de skad ces bka' stsald pa dang /bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa ded dpon la /tshe dang ldan ba sgra gcan z-in ky-is 'di skad ces gsold to //gang bcom ldan 'das de bzh-in gshegs pa dgra bcom ba
21b4(mdo): yang dag par rdzogs pa'-i sangs rgyas shag kya thub pa yongs su mya ngan las 'da'o zhes bgy-i ba'-i sgra de lta bu yang /bdag thos par myi spro na /bcom ldan 'das de yongs su mya ngan las 'da' ba mthong ba lta smos
21b5(mdo): kyang ci 'tshal //bcom ldan 'das gang bcom ldan 'das shag kya thub pa shag kya'i rgyal po da /yongs su mya ngan las 'da'o/zhes bgyi ba'i sgra de lta bu thos pas bdag lus dub par gyurd to //
21b6(mdo): bcom ldan 'das sems can r-in po che 'jig rten thams cad la phan par bzhed pa /'jig rten thams cad la phab dang /thugs rtse ba /'jig rten thams cad kyi sgron ma /'j-ig rten
21b7(mdo): thams cad dang my-i mtshungs pa /'jig rten thams can ky-i 'od mdzad pa /'jig rten thams cad kyi myig /shes rab kyi sgron ma chen por de lta bu dang /bdag sang nang par 'bral zh-ing 'bye ba dang /my-i
21b8(mdo): 'grogs par 'gyur ba ci ltar bgy-i /de skad ces gsold pa dang /bcom ldan 'das de bzh-in gshegs pa ded dpon kyis tshe dang ldan ba sgra gcan z-in la 'di skad ces bka' stsald to //sgra gcan zin thong /
21b9(mdo): smre sngags ma 'don /sgra gcan zin bcom ldan 'das des 'du byed thams cad myi rtag pa /'du byed thams cad sdug bsngal ba //chos thams cad bdag myed pa /mya ngan las 'das pa zh-i ba'o zhes thog ma 
22a1(mdo): /ny-id du chos ma bstand tam /sgra gcan zin bcom ldan 'das des /'du byed thams cad myi rtag ste /skye zhing 'jig pa'i chos can yin /skyes nas 'gag par 'gyur ba ste /de dag rnam p+Ra
22a2(mdo): zhi ba bde /zhes bya ba de skad kyang gsungs so //gsold pa /bcom ldan 'das de lta lags so /bde bar gshegs pa de lta lags so //bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsald pa /bcom ldan 'das
22a3(mdo): sdug pa dang /yid du 'ong ba thams cad dang /tha dad du 'gyur zhing 'gyes pa dang /bral ba dang /bye bar 'gyur ba dang /ji srid yun ring du gnas kyang nges par nyams par 'gyur ro zhes thog ma nyid du ma bstand tam /
22a4(mdo): gsold pa /bcom ldan 'as de lta lags so //bde bar gshegs pa de ltar lags so //bka' stsald pa /sgra gcan zan gang skyes shing 'byung ba 'dus byas pa /bsags pa /kun tu
22a5(mdo): rtog pa las byung ba /rtend cing 'breld par 'byung ba de dag 'jig par myi 'gyur ba de ga la yod de de ni gnas myed do //de nas tshe dang ldan ba sgra gcan zan /de bzh-in gshegs pa dgra
22a6(mdo): bcom ba yang dag par rdzogs pa'-i sangs rgyas yab shag kya thub pa de rjes su dran nas /chos ky-i shugs kyis mch-i ma byung bar gyurd to //bdag sang nang par bcom ldan 'das dge slong gi
22a7(mdo): dge 'dun gyis mdun tu bdar cing chos stond pa myi mthong ngo /ri 'i rgyal po ri rab rgya mtsho 'i nang nas mngon par byung zh-ing /lhag ger snang ba lta bu /zla ba nya ba skar mas yongs su bskord pa lta bu mtshan brgyas
22a8(mdo): brgyand pa /nyi ma 'od stong dang ldan ba nam mkha' la tha ler snang ba lta bu /rgya mtsho ltar zab c-ing tshad myed la /r-in po che du ma'i tshogs yod pa /'khor los sgyur ba 'i rgyal po ltar 'khor du mas yongs su bskor
22a9(mdo): pa /gangs r-i'i rgyal po ltar dbang po dang /stobs dang /byang chub kyi yan lag gi me tog kun tu rgyas pa /r-i 'khor yug chen po ltar phas ky-i rgol ba thams cad kyis rab tu my-i g.yos pa /pad ma ltar mtsho 'i nang
22b1(mdo): na 'dug kyang 'jig rten gy-i chos thams cad ky-is ma gos pa /tshangs pa 'khor kyi nang na tshangs pa lta bu /brgya byin myig stong dang ldan ba ltar seng ge'i bdan la bzhugs pa /seng ge ltar 'jigs shing skrag pa myed p+Ra /
22b2(mdo): seng ge 'i sgra yang dag par sgrogs pa /phy-ind cad bdag gis myi mthong ngo zhes /tshe dang ldan ba sgra gcan z-in ky-is de skad ces smras nas /ngu zhing sems khongs su chud de cang my-i smra bar 'dug go //de nas de bzhin 
22b3(mdo): gshegs pa ded dpon gy-is /tshe dang ldan ba sgra gachan zin la 'd-i skad ces bka' stsald to //sgra gcan zin khyod /bcom ldan 'das de '-i drung du song shig /bcom ldan 'das de khyod gzigs par bzhed kyis /
22b4(mdo): sgra gcan z-in khyod nga '-i ngag ma gcog par song shig /bcom ldan 'as de 'i sku 'o brgyal bar ma byed /sgra gcan zin khyod nges par de na 'dug dgos so //de ci 'i phyir zhe na /sgra gcan zind sangs 
22b5(mdo): rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams chos ny-id ky-is /de bzh-in gshegs pa byams pas khyab par mdzad pa dang /gang dgongs pa mdzad pa dang /gang zhig bzhed na de dag nges par yongs su mya 
22b6(mdo): ngan las my-i 'da' ste /de '-i tshe de dag nges par bzhugs par 'gyur ro /sgra gcan z-in de bzh-in gshegs pa de khyod gz-igs par bzhed kyis /song la khyod nges par de na gnas
22b7(mdo): par gyis shig //de nas tshe dang ldan ba sgra gcan z-ind kyis /bcom ldan 'das de bzh-in gshegs pa ded dpon gyi zhabs la mgo bos phyag 'tshal nas 'di lta ste dper na /skyes bu stobs dang ldan ba 
22b8(mdo): '-i lag pa bskum ba las brkyang ba 'am /brkyang ba las bskum ba de bzhin du tshe dang ldan ba sgra gcan c-in /bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom ba yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas 
22b9(mdo): ded dpon kyi sangs rgyas kyi zhing nas my-i snang bar gyurd te /ku sha'i grong khyer gyad rnams nye bar gnas pa'-i sh-ing sa la'-i tshal /shing sa la zung gi bar /bcom ldan 'das ga la ba der song ste phyind pa dang /
23a1(mdo): //bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la mgo bos phyag 'tshal nas /lan gsum bskor ba byas te /ngu zhing smre sngags 'dond /mchi ma 'byung bzhin du thal mo sbyar te /phyogs gcig tu 'dug go //
23a2(mdo): de nas bcom ldan 'das ky-is /tshe dang ldan ba sgra gcan zind la 'di skad ces bka' bstsald to /sgra gcan zin tshur shog /sgra gcan zind khyod myi dgar ma byed /ma ngu smra sngags ma 'don /yi ma chad cig /
23a3(mdo): sgra gcan zin khyod kyis pha la pha'i bya ba byas pa /ngas kyang khyod la bu'i bya ba byas so //sgra gcan zin de ltar ngas pha'i bya ba byas /khyod kyis kyang bu'i bya ba byas /bdag cag gnyi gas kyang bya ba byas shing /
23a4(mdo): byed pa byas zind kyis /sgra gcan zin khyed mya ngan ma byed /smre sngags ma 'don /y-i ma chad cig /sgra gcan zin bdag cag gnyis sems can thams cad 'j-igs pa myed par bya ba'i phyir 
23a5(mdo): zhugs pa dang /'khon myed pa dang /'gran zla myed pa dang /rnam par 'tho 'tsham ba myed pa dang /rnam par 'tshe ba myed par zhugs pa la ltos /nga yang da ni yongs su mya ngan las 'da' 
23a6(mdo): bar bya ba'i phyir gnas te /phyind cad su'-i phar yang myi 'gyur ro //sgra gcan zand khyod kyang yongs su mya ngan las 'das na /phy-ind cad su'i bur yang my-i 'gyur ro //sgra gcan zand 
23a7(mdo): bdag cag gny-is sems can thams cad la rnam par 'tshe ba myed pa dang /my-i mthun ba myed par gnas pa la ltos //de nas tshe dang ldan ba sgra gcan zin kyis /bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsold 
23a8(mdo): to //bcom ldan 'das yongs su mya ngan las my-i 'da' bar gsol //bde bar gshegs pa yongs su mya ngan las myi 'da' bar gsol /bcom ldan 'das skye bo mang po la smand pa dang /bde ba dang /
23a9(mdo): 'j-ig rten la thugs brtse ba dang /lha dang myi rnams ky-i don dang /phan ba dang bde ba'i slad du /bskald par bzhugs su gsol /de skad ces gsold pa dang /bcom ldan 'das ky-is tshe dang ldan ba sgra 
23b1(mdo): gcan z-ind la 'di skad ces bka' stsald to //sgra gcan zin chos gang dag dang ldan na 'jig rten na /de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom ba yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi grangs su 'gro ba'i chos de 
23b2(mdo): dag sgra gcan z-ind zad pa'am /rny-ings pa'am /skye ba'am /'gro ba'am /'ong ba 'am /'byung ba 'am /'gag pa 'am /srid pa 'am /'jig pa 'am /gnas pa 'am /'dug pa 'am /nyal 
23b3(mdo): ba 'am /ldan ba 'am /my-i ldan bar myi 'gyur ro //de ci 'i phyir zhe na /sgra gcan z-ind chos de ni gnas pa ste /sh-ind tu myi 'byung ba /shind tu myi skye ba /shind tu my-i 'gag pa /shin tu stong pa /
23b4(mdo): sh-ind tu skye ba myed pa /shind tu ngo bo nyid myed pa /nye bar zh-i ba /grangs myed pa /gzhi myed pa /brjod du myed pa /ngag gi lam ma yin ba'o //sgra gcan zind sangs rgyas bcom 
23b5(mdo): ldan 'das rnams kyi chos ny-id de /'di lta ste /shind tu gnas pa nyid dang /nye bar zh-i ba ny-id dang /sh-ind tu dben ba nyid dang /sh-ind tu 'dod chags dang bral ba ny-id dang /shind tu 
23b6(mdo): 'gag pa nyid dang /shind tu ma 'dres pa nyid dang /shind tu la byas pa ny-id dang /shind tu myi bzad pa nyid dang /sgra gcan zin ngas 'di la dgongs nas 'di skad du /de bzhin gshegs pa rnaMs
23b7(mdo): byung yang rung ste /de bzhin gshegs pa rnams ma byung yang rung ste /chos rnams kyi chos gnas pa nyid dang /chos nyid dang /chos nges pa nyid dang /chos 'dod chags dang bral ba nyid dang /chos kyi ngo bo nyid 
23b8(mdo): r-i gnas pa ny-id do zhes bshad do //sgra gcan zin de bzhin gshegs pa ni tshul khrims kyi phung po bsnams te /yongs su mya ngan las my-i 'da' /t-i nge 'dz-ind kyi phung po ma yin /shes rab kyi phung po ma
23b9(mdo): y-in /rnam par grol ba'i phung po ma yin /rnam par grol ba'i yais mthong ba'i phung po bsnams te /yong su mya ngan las myi 'das //sgra gcan zin khyod myi dgar ma byed /smre sngags ma 'don /
24a1(mdo): //y-i chad pa ma skyed c-ig //sgra gcan zind 'du byed thams cad ni myi rtag pa /myi brtan pa /dbugs 'byind pa myed pa /ther zug ma yin ba /zad cing 'gyur ba'i chos yin te /sgra gcan
24a2(mdo): zin de bas na 'du byed thams cad la y-id byung bar bya ba'i rigs /ma chags par bya ba'i rigs /rnam par grol bar bya ba'i rigs te /sgra gcan zin 'di ni khyod la rjes su bstand pa'o //sgra gcan zin dang /
24a3(mdo): bden ba mthong ba'i le 'u 'di /bcom ldan 'das ky-is bka' stsald pa /kund tu shes nas dge slong gnas brtan drug bcu tsam lend pa myed par zag pa rnams las sems rnam par grol lo //lha dang my-i
24a4(mdo): tshad myed pa dag chos la chos ky-i myig rdul myed cing dri ma dang bral ba rnam par dag pa'o //byang chub sems dpa' drug khr-i brgyad stong gyis ni myi skye ba'-i chos la bzod pa thob ste /thams cad kyang
24a5(mdo): kye ma'o sangs rgyas kye ma'o sangs rgyas /kye ma'o sangs rgyas ky-i chos bsam gyis myi khyab po zhes ched du brjod pa ched du brjod cing de dag gis /bcom ldan 'das la me tog ud pa la
24a6(mdo): dang /pad ma dang /ku mu ta dang /pad ma dkar po rnams kyis gtord /mngon par gtord nas tsh-ig 'di skad ces bdag cag kyang ma 'ongs pa'i dus na /stond pa 'di lta bur 'jig rten
24a7(mdo): du byung bar gyurd cig /'di ltar chos stond par gyurd cig //bla na myed pa 'jig rten thams cad dang /myi 'dra ba'i yongs su mya ngan las 'da' ba /sangs rgyas kyi yongs su mya ngan las 'das
24a8(mdo): ba chen po 'di lta bus yongs su mya ngan las 'da'o bar gyurd cig ces smras so //de skad ces smras nas byang chub sems dpa' de dag cang myi smra bar gyurd to ////sgra gcan zind kyi
24a9(mdo): le'u ste /bzhi pa rdzogs s+ho //////de 'i tshe tshe dang ldan ba kun dga' bo /bcom ldan 'das kyi phang na gz-ims khr-i la bsnyes nas /du ste mch-i ma phyung smre sngags 'don
24b1(mdo): c-ing 'dug go //de nas tshe dang ldan ba kun dga' bo /mtshaM ma zag par ngu zhing shing ljon pa chen po bcad de bsgyeld pa bzhin du 'gyel te /bcom ldan 'das yongs su mya ngan las 'da' ba ha cang myur ches so

24b3(mdo): so /mar mye chen po 'jig rten na myi snang bar 'gyur ba ha cang myur ces so //sgron ma chen po 'jig rten na myi nang bar 'gyur ba ha cang myir ches so //'j-ig rten gyi mchog 'jig rten na 'gag par 'gyur
24b4(mdo): ba ha cang myur ches so //sems can rin po che /sems can pad ma dkar po /'jig rten na myi snang bar gyurd pa ha cang myur ches so / //sems can cang shes pa /sems can dul ba /sems can
24b5(mdo): ma dul ba dul ba 'jig rten na myed par 'gyur ba ha cang m+y+rur ches so /ded dpon stond pa po bde ba'-i lam stond pa /'jig rten na my-i snang bar 'gyur ba ha cad myur ches so //myig

24b7(mdo): rten long bar 'gyur ba ha cang myur ces so //bcom ldan 'das 'jig rten mgon ma mchis pa /dang /yongs su 'drend pa ma mch-is par 'gyur ba ha cang myer ches so //sang nang par nas mying
24b8(mdo): mtshan tsam zhig lus te /sems can r-in po tshe my-i mthong bar 'gyur ba lta /bcom ldan 'das kyis /tshe dang ldan ba kun dga' bo la bka' bstsald pa /kun dga' bo thong mya ngan ma byed /
24b9(mdo): smre sngags ma 'don cig /kun dga' bo sdug pa dang /y-id du 'ong ba thams cad dang tha dad par 'gyur zhing /'gyes par 'gyur ba dang /bye bar 'gyur ba dang /bral bar 'gyur bar ngas thog ma nas
25a1(mdo): //ma bshad dam /kun dga' bo gang skyes pa byung ba /'dus byas pa /bsags pa /rtend c-ing 'breld par 'byung ba /'jig pa'i chos can de my-i 'jig par ga la 'gyur te /de ni gnas
25a2(mdo): myed do //de lta bur gyurd pa dag gnas shing myi snang bar myi 'gyur ba de ni gnas myed do //kun dga' bo ci ltar ring du gnas kyang nges par yongs su mya ngan las 'da' ba'i chos can du 'gyur ro //de bas na kun
25a3(mdo): dga' bo khyod mya ngan ma byed /smre sngags ma 'dond cig //de nas tshe dang ldan ba kun dga' bo myig myi 'dzums par /bcom ldan 'das kyi zhal du bltas nas shing ljon pa bcad de /sgyeld pa bzhin du yang
25a4(mdo): sa la 'gyeld to //bcom ldan 'das kyis tshe dang ldan ba kun dga' bo la lan gny-is su bka' stsald pa /kun dga' bo thong mya ngan ma byed /smre sdags ma 'dond cig /kun dga' bo
25a5(mdo): don 'di mya ngan byas pa dang /smre sngags 'don pas 'don pa sa rnyed par sla ba ma yin no /sdug pa dang /y-id du 'ong ba thams cad dang /tha dad du 'gyur zhing 'gyes par 'gyur bar
25a6(mdo): ngas thog ma nas bshad do //'du byed rnams c-i srid ring du gnas kyang /nges par rnam par 'gyur ba'i chos can yin no //kun dga' bo khyod kyis de bzhin gshegs pa la byams pa'i
25a7(mdo): lus kyi las phan ba dang /bde ba dang /gnyis su myed pa dang /tshad myed pa dang /'khon myed pa dang /'gran zla myed pa dang /byams pa'-i ngag g-i las dang /byams pa'-i y-id ky-i las bde ba nas
25a8(mdo): 'gran zla myed pa'-i bar gyis bsnyen bkur byas so //de nas tshe dang ldan ba kun dga' bo sa las langs nas mchi ma phyis te /bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsold to //bcom ldan 'das bdag
25a9(mdo): sems can rin po che de lta bu dang /tha dad du 'gyur//bdag thugs rje chen po'i 'j-ig rten thams cad las 'phags pa /'jig rten thams cad la phan bar bzhed pa /'j-ig rten thams cad la thugs
25b1(mdo): brtse /brtse ba can 'jig rten thams cad la gces pa 'jig rten thams cad kyi stond pa /'jig rten thams cad kyi yongs su 'drend pa /'j-ig rten thams cad la phan ba dang /bde ba sbyin ba sems 
25b2(mdo): can chen po de lta bu dang /sang nang par 'bral zhing tha dad du 'gyur na /ci 'i slad du bdag mya ngan myi bgyid /smre sngags my-i 'don /de nas tshe dang ldan ba kun dga' bo mchi ma mang du phyung nas /mchi 
25b3(mdo): ma phyis te yang tshig 'di skad ces smras so //kye ma'o gang du yang mar me chen po /sgron ma chen po snang ba chen po 'di lta bu 'jig rten na myi snang bar 'gyur /ye shes kyi nyi ma chen po 'od zer bye ba khrag 
25b4(mdo): khr-ig brgya stong du mas bskord pa /kun tu 'od /kun tu snang ba /kun tu lam me ba /kun tu 'od zer /kun tu rtog /kun tu gzigs pa /kun tu shes rab /kun tu dran ba /kun tu spyod yul /
25b5(mdo): kun tu 'od kyi sems can /shes rab kyi snang ba chen po 'di lta ba'i 'j-ig rten na nub par 'gyur ba'-i 'du byed rnams ni g.yo can no //brnyas thabs can no //kye ma'o 'jig rten na 'od 
25b6(mdo): byed pa myi snang bar 'gyur ba ha cang myur ches so //lha dang bcas pa'i 'jig rten kyi mgon po myi snang bar 'gyur ba ha cing myur ches so //kye ma'o bcom ldan 'das cho 'phrul dang bcas pa
25b7(mdo): 'jig rten na myi snang bar gyur ba ha cang myur ches na /bcom ldan 'das ci 'i slad du bdag mya ngan myi bgyid /bcom ldan 'das ci'i slad du bdag smre sngags myi 'don /bcom ldan 'das bdag snying rnam
25b8(mdo): pa brgyar ma gas pa ngo mtshar che 'o /bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan sngar ma gum pa ngo mtshar che'o //bcom ldan 'das bdag de la 'di snyam du bcom ldan 'das kyi byin kyi brlabs kyis /bdag ma gum mo 
25b9(mdo): snyam bgyid do //de ci 'i slad du zhe na /bcom ldan 'das las bdag g-is gang chos kyi phung po brgyad khr-i bzhi stong mngon tu mnos /mngon tu kun chub par bgy-is /mngon tu kun bzung ba de dag /da dung skye bo 
26a1(mdo): //mang po la phan bar ma gyurd /phyogs mang por rgyas par ma gyurd pas /bcom ldan 'das bdag 'di snyam du bdag ma gum ba de ni /bcom ldan 'das kyi mthu lags pa snyam bgyid de /
26a2(mdo): bcom ldan 'das bdag ci 'i slad du mya ngan myi bgyid /bcom ldan 'das bdagaci 'i slad du smre sngags my-i 'dond /bcom ldan 'das bad grong khyer ser skya 'i gnas su /bcom ldan 'das bltam ba'i sar 
26a3(mdo): mchis nas shag kya rnams 'tshogs pa la /shag kya thub pa shag kya'i rgyal po shind tu myi 'byung bar 'gags so /zhes bgyi 'am /c-i skad bgy-i /rgyal po 'i khab kyi grong khyer du mch-is nas /lus 'phags mo'i 
26a4(mdo): bu /rgyal po ma skyes dgra la yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas nyi ma chen po nub ste /mtshams myed pa'i las kyi zug rngu 'byind pa /sman pa'i rgyal po chen po de gshegs lags so /
26a5(mdo): zhes bgyi'am /ci skad bgyi /grong khyer chen po mnyan du yod du mchis nas /thugs rje chen po brnyes pa phan pa bzhed pa /'jig rten la thugs brtse ba can de my-i snang bar gyurd to /zhes
26a6(mdo): bgyi 'am /c-i skad bgyi /mgon myed zas sbyin la /de bzhin gshegs pa rgyal byed kyi tshal /mgon myed zas sbyin gyi kun dga' ra bar j-ig /bzhugs par 'gyur ram /zhes bgy-i 'am /j-i skad 
26a7(mdo): bgy-i /yangs pa'i grong khyer chen por mch-is nas /l-i tsa byi 'i tshogs rnams kyi mdun tu ded dpon phan par bzhed pa de nub par gyurd to //zhes bgy-i 'am /c-i skad bgyi /phyogs dang /phyogs mtshams 
26a8(mdo): rnams nas /rigs kyi bu dang /r-igs ky-i bu mo gang dag zhu ba yongs su zhu ba '-i slad du mchi ba de rnams la /mkhyend pa chen po dang ldan ba /'j-ig rten thams cad ky-i khams mkhyend pa mkhas pa /the 
26a9(mdo): tsom zhus pa /thams cad seld pa /de nub par gyurd to zhes bgyi 'am /c-i skad bgyi'am /phyogs dang phyogs mtshams rnams nas /dbyar gyi gnas su gnas gnas pa'-i dge slong /bcom 
26b1(mdo): ldan 'das la blta ba dang /phyag 'tshal ba dang /bsnyen bkur bgyi ba dang /zhu ba dang /brtag pa'i slad du mch-i ba de rnams la chos dang ldan ba'i gtam gsung zhing myi 'i chos bla ma rtogs pa /bcom ldan
26b2(mdo): 'das my-i bzhugs na /bdag gis myi mthong ngo //myi thos so /cho 'phrul dang bcas pa'i tshangs par spyod pa de lta bu 'jig rten na myi snang bar gyurd na /bcom ldan 'das      bdag ci 'i slad du mya ngan myi
26b3(mdo): bgyid /ci 'i slad du bdag smre sngags myi 'don /de skad ces gsold pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis tshe dang ldan ba kun dga' bo la 'di skad ces bka' stsald to /kun dga' bo thong /mya ngan ma byed /
26b4(mdo): smre sngags ma 'dond c-ig /kun dga' bo nga 'i tshangs par spyod pa /skye bo mang po par gyurd pa /lha dang /myi rnams la rgya che zh-ing yangs pa dang /lha dang bcas pa'i 'jig rten gyis mnos pa /yun
26b5(mdo): ring du gnas par 'gyur ro //kun dga' bo nga yongs su mya ngan las 'das nas dge slong 'od srung dang /kun dga' bo khyod gnyis lo bzhi bcu lhag tsam du cho 'phrul dang /bcas pa'-i
26b6(mdo): tshangs par spyod pa /lha dang myis so sor mnos pa de spyod par 'gyur gyis /kun dga' bo khyod mya ngan ma byed /smre sngags ma 'dond c-ig/nga 'i dam pa'i chos skye bo mang po par
26b7(mdo): gyurd pa /lha dang my-i rnams la rgya che zh-ing yang par gyurd pa yun ring du gnas par 'gyur ro //kun dga' bo nga 'das nas dge slong 'od srung dang /khyed gnyis bskald pa grangs myed pa bye ba khrag
26b8(mdo): khr-ig brgya stong du yang dag par bsgrubs pa'-i bla na myed pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub 'd-i dang /nga '-i dge ba'i chos dkon mchog 'd-i rnam par 'phel zh-ing yongs su my-i nyams par bya ba'-i phyir zhugs
26b9(mdo): par 'gyur ro //de ci'i phy-ir zhe na /kun dga' bo dge slong 'od srung ni 'dod pa chung ba /rab tu dben ba /brtson 'grus brtsams pa /dran ba nye bar gzhag pa /spros pa myed pa la dga' ba /
27a1(mdo): //mnyam bar bzhag pa shes rab rno ba'o //kun dga' bo dge slong 'od srung ni skye bo mang po la yang dag par stond pa /yang dag par 'dz-ind tu 'jug pa /yang dag par gzeng bstod pa /yang
27a2(mdo): dag par rab tu dga' bar byed pa /tshangs pa mtshungs par spyod pa thams cad la /chos 'chad pas ngal ba myed pa'o //kun dga' bo dge slong 'od srung ni 'khor bzhi po dag g-i pha ma lta bu'o //kun dga'
27a3(mdo): bo dge slong 'od srung n-i 'j-ig rten la yun ring bar lta zh-ing snying rtse ba can no //kun dga' bo dge slonga'ado srung ni skye bo mang po la phan ba dang /skye bo mang po la bde ba dang /'j-ig rten la snying brtse ba dang /lha
27a4(mdo): dang myi rnam kyi don dang /phan ba dang /bde ba'i phy-ir zhugs pa yin no //gsold pa /bcom ldan 'das dge slong 'od srung skye bo mang po ci snyed cig gi don la dga' ba dang /lha dang my-i rnams la phan
27a5(mdo): pa dang /bde bar dga' ba la zhugs pa lags /bka' stsald pa /kun dga' bo dge slong 'od srung gang gi tshe /yongs su mya ngan las 'da' ba de 'i tshe /'di ltar byin gyis rlob
27a6(mdo): par 'gyur te /bdag yongs su mya ngan las 'das nas /de bzh-in gshegs pa dgra bcom ba yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas byams pa 'jig rten du ma byung gi bar du /bdag
27a7(mdo): g-i lus gzhan du ma gyurd cig /gos dang /spu dang /skra dang /pags pa dang /mdog dang /dbang po rnams gzhan du ma gyurd c-ig //bdag gi lus sam /yan lag thams cad kyang gzhan du ma

27a9(mdo): gsum pa dang bcas pas bdag gi lus ma zhig par gzigs par gyurd cig //bdag gis chos bshad pa la nye bar brtend pa dang /bdag gi byin kyi rlabs dang /bdag la dmy-igs pas srog chags

27b2(mdo): po dbang po ma yengs pa /gos ci lta ba bzhin du bgos pa de rnams kyis bdag gi lus ma zhig par gzigs par gyurd pa de 'i tshe /bdag gi lus nam mkha' la mngon par 'phags nas /lus kyi

27b4(mdo): smy-ind par bya ba'i phyir de ltar skye bo mang po la phan ba dang /de ltar skye bo mang po la bde ba 'i phyir zhugs pa yin no //kun dga' bo dge slong 'od srung yongs su mya ngan las 'das nas

28a1(mdo): //khrag dang /pags pa dang /de las gzhan ba'-i yan lag dang /ny-ing lag rnams dang /dbang po rnams dang /gos rnams gzhan du my-i 'gyur zhing /lus dang dr-ing ba myed par 'gyur ro //kun

28a9(mdo): pa dang /bde ba'i phy-ir zhugs pa la ltos /lan gny-is su yang de bzh-in gshegs pa byams pa nyan thos bye ba dgu bcu rtsa bzh-i dang /thabs gcig sa phyogs der gshegs par 'gyur ro //kun dga' bo lan

30a9(mdo): 'gyur ro //der dgra bcom ba khr-i sum stong 'dus par 'gyurd te /thams cad kyang rdzu 'phrul che ba /mthu che ba /dbang che bar grags pa nas rgya cher tshangs pa mtshungs par spyod pa rnams la chos 'chad pas ngal

30b2(mdo): spyod pa rnams la chos 'chad pas ngal ba myed pa'i bar du ste /de yang nga '-i tshangs par spyod pa /cho 'phrul dang bcas pa /lha dang myi rnams la rgyas par byed par 'gyur gyis /kun dga' bo khyod mya ngan ma byed /smre
30b3(mdo): sngags ma 'dond cig /kun dga' bo nga 'das nas /grong khyer gser kyi bre zhes bya ba na /bram ze'i rigs las rab tu byung ba dge slong mkhas pa zhes bya ba dang /yang dag par rgyal ba can zhes bya ba gnyis

30b5(mdo): dga' bo khyod mya ngan ma bred /smre sngags ma 'dond cig /kun dga' bo nga 'das nas/gro khyer gnas bcas zhes bya ba na dge slong brtson 'grus che zhes bya ba 'byung ste /rdzu 'phrul
30b6(mdo): che ba /mthu che ba de yang tshangs par spyod pa rnams la cho 'phrul dang bcas par byed par 'gyur gyi /kun dga' bo khyod myi ngan ma byed /smre sngags ma 'don cig //kun dga' bo nga 'das
30b7(mdo): nas /ma 'ongs pa'i dus na dge slong nyi ma'i gung zhes bya ba 'byung ste /kun dga' bo dge slong nyi ma'i gung de yang nga 'i chos 'dul ba 'di rgyas par byed c-ing /lha dang my-i rnams la cho 'phrul dang bachas pa dang /

30b9(mdo): 'od srung zhes bya ba 'byung ste /rdzu 'phrul che ba mthu che ba dag che bar grags pa /gsal ba /'jigs pa myed pa /mang du thos pa /'dul ba 'dz-ind pa /mdo 'dz-ind pa /ma mo 'dzind pa /yang dag par
31a1(mdo): //stond pa /yang dag par 'dzind tu 'jug pa /yang dag par gzeng bstod pa /yang dag par rab tu dga' bar byed pa nas /de yang nga '-i bstand pa rgyas par byed pa'-i bar du 'gyur gyis /kun

31a5(mdo): ba byed pa dang /rjed pa la spro bar 'gyur te /rim kyis bya nga chub kyi tshogs yongs su rdzogs par byas nas /ma 'ongs pa'-i dus na bskald pa stong rdzogs pa dang /bla na myed pa yang
31a6(mdo): dag par rdzogs pa'-i byang chub mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya bar 'gyur te /bskald pa bzang byed ces bya ba la bkod pa chen pos legs par brgyan pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams su kun tu

31b1(mdo): du bya ba dang /bla mar bya ba dang /rim 'gror bya ba dang /mchod pa dang /rjed pa dang /ri mor bya ba la spro bar 'gyur ro //der yang nga y-i nyan thos rdzu 'phrul che ba /mthu che ba /dbang che bar grags pa brgya phrag
